**Stable:** Owner merged IP01 [#160](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/160) to `ios-prep` `97cf88a403a870ecab668864d0778849ba9ffcf0`; [post-merge stable Actions #44968131740](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/actions/runs/44968131740) **SUCCESS**. Owner says they are **testing IP01** and will provide results: **merged, OWNER DEVICE TEST PENDING**. The earlier IP01 checklist below remains historical and relevant to this unfinished acceptance; don't mark it PASS without explicit owner evidence.

**[New IP02 PR #161](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/161) OPEN / UNMERGED, branch `fix/ip02-daily-next-ready-todo-mode-lock`:** Two functional fixes on an unmerged branch; exact PR-head CI and physical iPad/iPhone **NOT TESTED** at documentation authoring. No Swift, native plugin, privacy, profile, reward or gameplay speed changes. The code-based hypothesis is that a correct Daily Question answer shows an immediately clickable Next while a 900 ms star popup later **replaces the entire slide**, potentially losing a WebKit touch crossing the old/new DOM. New Next is visually disabled/has `aria-busy` until feedback settles (900 ms star, 320 ms otherwise). The existing Next element is then enabled **without rerendering**; early taps are ignored, single-question answer/star credit and Next transitions remain one-time. A paused Welcome Back cancels feedback without leaving an already-answered Next permanently disabled.

**Shared menu behavior:** The existing bridge `isDailyTodoLaunch()` validates full Daily context (profile, plan, day, task, launch token, verse, activity kind/id, assigned mode for games, return context); the shared `gameMenuHtml()` hides Mode Select only for this valid context or for existing Game Mix, and a click-time check prevents stale button callbacks from switching modes. Normal direct practice launch, legacy `zoo_todo`, and incomplete Daily context still allow Mode Select; individually suppressed menus stay suppressed. `daily-todo-shell.js` assigned-mode autoselect/return processing is **unchanged**.

**Automated regression:** `node scripts/validate_ip02_daily_next_mode.js` added to `.github/workflows/ios-prep-validation.yml`. Executes the real answer handler, timer, Next and page transition under fake timers/DOM; asserts only one correct-answer star with rapid taps, expired animation doesn't replace an enabled Next, Next is locked until ready, one advance despite duplicate taps, no credit for wrong answer, and interrupted feedback cancellation. Exercises real bridge Daily-context validator and shared Game Menu across all 12 games + 5 Playground apps, ordinary/legacy/incomplete/Game Mix contexts, plus a stale Mode Select callback. Existing `npm run validate:daily-zoo-todo` checks assigned-mode selection and successful Daily completion credits separately. **Report final exact-head Node/Web/Swift-source results after Actions; not a physical native Build/Archive.**

**Owner on-device acceptance checklist (iPad AND iPhone):**
1. Daily Questions normal play: answer correctly and receive first/second star, then answer correctly after star cap; separately answer incorrectly. Next appears faded/unavailable during feedback, becomes fully enabled after its visible feedback, and the **first enabled tap** changes to next question. Fast double-tap or tap at the 900 ms boundary must not skip a question or award a duplicate star/peg.
2. Daily Questions with the final question: Next routes once to Feeding in ordinary play. In actual Daily To-Do Questions context: the final action must return to the assigned To-Do task and mark it complete **once**, not start an extra Feeding game. Answer/audio/verse-help and all previously completed question data remain intact.
3. During an answer's star animation, hold the iOS App Switcher, return, pass the genuine **Welcome Back** gesture, then press Next. It should be ready, require one explicit tap, not silently advance, not auto-play audio or make duplicate rewards. Also test incorrect answer or muted audio.
4. Launch a real **Tower of Bible** and **Bible Bugs** Daily To-Do game at its assigned difficulty; open Menu: **Mode Select absent**, while Help/Mute/Exit/Close work. Repeat on at least one other Practice Game and one Playground activity from Daily To-Do. The selected task mode, activity exit, credits/medals, and return route must be unchanged. Then launch the same games **normally outside Daily To-Do**: Mode Select still appears and works. Game Mix remains independently locked.
5. Check **iPad portrait/landscape and iPhone narrow/typical portrait/landscape**. The faded Next state must not resize/reflow the button or clip text; Menu remains unchanged except missing Mode Select on Daily runs. Verify reduced-motion, Web/PWA/Android smoke when available, score single-credit, sound/Welcome Back and no profile data loss.

**Record actual owner PASS/FAIL/NOT TESTED** per tested scenario, exact device/OS/version if provided. Owner controls the PR merge and native Xcode build. Existing local family data must never be wiped, imported or reinstalled for this test (10 production backups and 11 independent Audio Test backups; Zoo surprise/rescue storage omitted by family-v2). Report IP01 test results separately when provided, update this file and **same canonical handoff** in the next PR, not silently.

## HISTORICAL — WHEEL-01 candidate before #158 owner iPad PASS (superseded)

**Owner reproduction / bug:** In native iPad Wheel of Bible, opening Game Menu freezes a running raining Bible Bux animation correctly. Muting from the menu and closing it can leave the game clock/animation permanently paused; the menu button then rejects reopening even though task-switching still shows a responsive Welcome Back. Report reproduced; no Xcode JavaScript stack trace was captured.

**Code explanation / proposed correction:** The Wheel menu's `onOpen` sets `nativeWheelMenuOpen=true` before `pauseWheelForReturn()`. In the muted `primeWheelNativeFromGesture("resume")` path, the old `!nativeWheelMenuOpen` guard suppresses both unlock and `resumeWheelAfterReturn()`, so `welcomePaused` remains true and the next menu tap is ignored. The unmuted path already clears the lock on successful preflight. New `fix/ipad-wheel-menu-mute-resume` makes muted Close first clear `nativeWheelMenuOpen` and then restore the original animation and timer ownership without sound activation. No change to native plugins, AVAudioSession or gameplay payout.

**Targeted automated check:** `node scripts/validate_wheel_menu_mute_resume.js` (also in `ios-prep-validation.yml`): loads the actual production Wheel menu/prime/pause/resume function bodies under a fake DOM/clock and checks muted Close resumes timers and animations, no native session activation while muted, next menu opens, and unmuting then closing preserves native preflight/reentry. **Test and final PR-head CI result not yet recorded at authoring; do not label PASS before actual execution.** GitHub Node/Web/Swift grammar CI never replaces Apple's Xcode build or on-device animation observation.

**Focused owner iPad test AFTER owner-reviewed merge and explicit device build:**
1. In Wheel of Bible, reach the **raining Bible Bux** moment. Open Game Menu: animation/time should pause. Tap Mute, Close: rain/timers should resume *without sound*. Immediately reopen Game Menu; button must respond.
2. While the menu is open, tap Unmute and Close: game resumes and intended audio works after native preflight. Repeat Mute → Close and reopen several times (including toggling twice inside one menu); no locked button, frozen payout, duplicate Bible Bux or skipped step.
3. Repeat normal menu open/close **without** toggling mute during spinning, verse/prize and result transitions. The game must resume; no accidental cash/score changes, duplicate tones or lost input.
4. While mid-game, hold App Switcher or switch apps and return. Genuine Welcome Back still appears and its real gesture safely resumes (or offers an honest retry when external media owns audio). Menu/Help/Exit remain reachable; do not auto-resume while backgrounded.
5. Record actual iPad model/iPadOS/audio route and the steps personally tried as **PASS / FAIL / NOT TESTED**. The uploaded Xcode debug trace has normal bridge audio traffic and lifecycle events, but no menu-specific JS exception that would independently prove this fix works.

**Safety:** Test against an existing owner-approved build; never uninstall, import/restore family backups, clear local storage or infer data preservation from CI. Keep 10 original production Zookeepers separate from 11 Audio Test profiles; standalone Zoo rescue/surprise stats remain outside family-v2 exports.

## HISTORICAL R16b.3 pre-merge device checklist (superseded by #153 owner report)

**Corrective PR status:** [R16b.3 PR #153](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/153) is **OPEN, not merged**, targeting only `integration/native-audio-to-ios-prep`. CI must pass on the final exact head; physical iPhone acceptance **NOT TESTED** for this correction. The old [#152](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/152) is **MERGED**, with owner-reported Silent-ON failure recorded below.

**Physical iPhone findings on merged [PR #152](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/152):** #152 **MERGED** to `integration/native-audio-to-ios-prep` at `6b23ff8fed0f`. Owner reports **Silent OFF: original music and verse on start; button OFF silences only music while verse continues — PASS for this observed behavior**. **Silent ON: initial music AND verse are silent; button OFF makes verse audible but no music — FAIL for the owner's newly clarified intended default.** Owner also reports the PNG music notes need to be **smaller within the existing yellow button**. Do not mistake merged source/CI status for owner acceptance.

**Owner's clarified product policy supersedes the narrower R15 optional-music rule for this activity only:** Verse Crawl music is now an intentional **explicit exception**. With **either iPhone Silent switch position**, a new Crawl session starts **Music ON**, with **both theme and spoken verse audible**. A tap on the top-right `#ffc751` music button fades it and turns **only soundtrack/ending OFF**; verse audio continues uninterrupted. Another tap restores music, with no verse pause/restart, no popup, no stored preference, and no Silent-switch detection. Smaller PNG artwork is inset within the same 44–56px accessible tap target. Other BibloZoo game/UI sounds must continue respecting Silent Mode.

**R16b.3 technical boundary:** On the actual child Start and explicit Welcome Back, iOS native Read may request a scoped `read-crawl` app-wide `.playback` lease. The native plugin admits **only source-validated Crawl theme, ending and published verse audio** under it; all incidental UI/typing/Zoo sounds and generated notes are excluded. No extra standalone verse lease is taken, and toggling music does **not** modify the category or interrupt/restart speech. The lease ends on Exit/`stopAll`, true OS interruption, route loss and media reset; no background mode, auto-play-after-foreground, Apple Music takeover, private data writes, mic or profile change. Web/PWA/Android keep their normal platform audio rules and soundtrack-only toggle.

**Verification:** R16b.3 adds `scripts/validate_r16b3_crawl_silent_mode_session.js`, revises the earlier R16b/R16b.2 regression, and runs full GitHub Actions including Swift source parsing and production web build. **Physical iPhone outcome for this corrective change: NOT TESTED** until the owner merges and runs the focused test. Run **Silent ON and OFF separately:** (1) default Music ON = theme+verse audible; (2) tap OFF during actual verse speech = theme silent, same voice continues; (3) tap ON = music returns, same voice continues; (4) rapid taps, before/after opening fanfare, final ending, completion and restart reset; (5) lock/held App Switcher/PiP/Apple Music and optional AirPods route; genuine interruptions require explicit Welcome Back and audio-clock validation. Record PASS/FAIL/NOT TESTED per item; no assumption of Xcode SDK build or acoustic proof from CI.

**Branch/owner gate:** `fix/r16b3-verse-crawl-music-on-silent-mode` targets only `integration/native-audio-to-ios-prep`. Do **not** merge without owner review or promote to `ios-prep`/`main`; R17 broad acceptance remains separate. Protect original 10 family profiles, separate 11 Audio Test profiles, all RAM recordings and off-phone backups.

## HISTORICAL R16b.2 implementation before owner Silent ON test (merged #152; superseded by R16b.3)

**Owner design approved for implementation, NOT yet iPhone-tested:** Verse Crawl starts with its original music and spoken verse; a top-right circular `#ffc751` button uses the owner's newly uploaded `verse_images/game_ui/ui_icon_music_notes.png`. Tap it once to fade the button and choose **music OFF / spoken verse only**. Tap again to restore **music ON / original music + narration**. No startup popup, hardware Silent-switch detection, global mute change, or saved preference. Each session starts in original music mode.

**Native safety boundary:** Music ON preserves the original `.soloAmbient` soundtrack/verse overlap (both respond to iPhone Silent Mode). Music OFF stops both Crawl theme and ending, awaits stop acknowledgements, and only then starts source-validated verse speech under a short-lived `.playback` essential-voice lease, so verse narration can be heard with Silent ON while optional music is never carried into the lease. Switching during a verse retires only its current player and restarts the same verse clip from its last known position; reversing the toggle stops that lease before restoring the original music. The existing interruption, external-media priority, no-autoplay, and explicit Welcome Back rules remain; no WebAudio fallback or new permissions.

**Verification gate:** New `scripts/validate_r16b2_crawl_music_toggle.js` (source contracts plus actual native JS adapter and toggle VM) is wired into GitHub Actions with prior R16a/R16b/Read/Swift-parse/web-build regressions. **Physical-iPhone testing is NOT YET DONE.** The owner must try both button directions during narration with speaker Silent ON/OFF, recovery and Exit, and make sure no false Welcome Back, audible leaked music, truncated progress or false reward occurs. CI alone cannot establish physical audio correctness. [Scoped device checklist](TESTING.md) and [focused decision history](R16B2-VERSE-CRAWL-SILENT-MODE-DECISION.md).

**PR status:** [R16b.2 PR #152](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/152) is OPEN (initially draft), **not merged**. Automated checks must pass on the final exact PR head; owner iPhone result is still pending.

**Release discipline:** Implement on short-lived branch `feat/r16b2-verse-crawl-music-toggle` targeting only `integration/native-audio-to-ios-prep`. Leave `ios-prep`, `main`, and the 10 original production / 11 separate Audio Test profiles intact. R17 final owner acceptance/promotion remains blocked until this change is merged and physically verified. The older R16b.2 three-option decision notes below are historical, superseded by this owner-approved toggle design.

### R16b.2 owner physical iPhone test — run AFTER merge (PENDING)

On the **actual installed integration build**, without uninstall/reset or touching private family JSON, check the following separately as **PASS / FAIL / NOT TESTED**, including speaker route and iOS version if known. These checks are not inferred from Node/source validation:

- **Silent OFF, initial Music ON:** Theme and published verse both play; top-right owner music-note image is bright yellow `#ffc751`; no popup. Tap OFF **during speech**: soundtrack stops, button fades, the same spoken clip continues/restarts near its last position without repeating verse credit or showing Welcome Back. Tap ON during speech: music returns and verse remains correct. Repeat twice; no restart of animation or duplicate completion.
- **Silent ON, initial Music ON:** As before, theme and verse respect Silent Mode (quiet). Tap OFF: **verse narration becomes audible, music/ending remain silent**. Tap ON: iOS may silence both again; tap OFF once more and verify spoken verse is audible. Check each toggle in the reference/fanfare period and during later chunks.
- **Completion and recovery:** Music OFF never plays the theme or ending while a verse owns the essential voice lease. Finish a complete run once per mode; no missed/duplicated words, reward or Zoo stats. Exit and start again: initial music state resets to ON. Lock/held App Switcher and external Apple Music/PiP while voice-only is playing must stop audio and freeze progress; explicit **Welcome Back / Let's go** restores only after verified voice-clock progress, never automatically. Try quick ON/OFF taps and AirPods if practical; log untested cases accurately.
- **Safety/non-regression:** Learn Chunks/Echo Menu, Flashcards capture and RAM replay, Typewriter, Balloons/Fish, and web/PWA/Android still behave according to their preexisting contracts. No developer logs, voice recordings, private profiles or family backup exports uploaded to PR.

## HISTORICAL R16b.1 proposal and pre-fix owner report / AUDIO-01 — owner Typewriter regression, targeted fix under review (2026-11-18)

**Merged baseline:** [R16b PR #149](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/149) merged into `integration/native-audio-to-ios-prep` as `60f187a57626710e98ae7c325b5376d1160190de`; [post-merge CI 44387158750](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/actions/runs/44387158750) **PASS**. Owner device test of that **merged** version reveals a specific new failure: **Typewriter Silent ON** key clicks quiet, but an unwanted **Welcome Back** appears before each verse segment; voice plays after the child's Let's go tap. **Typewriter Silent OFF** ordinary key clicks audible but the *same false Welcome Back* before each spoken segment. No other Read activity, interruption, headphone route, or explicit OS version test is inferred.

**Source-backed diagnosis:** Typewriter's final typing alert intentionally overlaps the start of spoken narration (`playChunkAlertSound(); finishCurrentChunk()`; `stopKeySoundVoices({keepChunkAlert:true})`). R16b #149 made essential `read-narration` refuse `.playback` if *any* native Read audio clip was still running; its `voice-overlap-unsafe` refusal triggers the existing Read Welcome Back safety path even in Silent OFF. This exact mechanism is strongly supported by source but **no Xcode reason trace was supplied for this run**, so require post-fix physical iPhone verification.

**R16b.1 proposed repair:** Branch `fix/r16b1-read-typewriter-optional-key-handoff` preempts **only** fixed-source short Typewriter/Keyboard key and final-alert clips, after spoken verse has prepared and **before** its brief `.playback` lease. It never preempts Crawl music/ending, Zoo cue, other verse clips, unknown audio or scheduled Read notes; all such overlap still fails closed. Optional clips emit truthful `interrupted` (not a fake `ended` or OS lifecycle notification), so source owners can replay future keys. Global UI effects remain silent on physical Silent-ON speaker. Short end-of-chunk alert may be truncated when the verse starts. All original foreground/OS interruptions, profile/recording privacy and no background/audio auto-resume stay intact.

**Next gate:** Focused `scripts/validate_r16b1_typewriter_effect_voice_handoff.js`, adjusted R16a/R16b tests, all native/Read regressions, Swift syntax and web build must **PASS for exact proposed head**. After owner review/merge, repeat Typewriter ON and OFF to verify no false popup and real speech; then one genuine lock/App Switcher interruption must **still** cause Welcome Back. Quick Balloons/Fish and Silent-OFF Crawl checks. **R16b Crawl voice-under-Silent is NOT solved, R17 remains blocked.** Full [R16b.1 diagnosis and owner test plan](R16B1-TYPEWRITER-VOICE-HANDOFF.md). No auto-merge; no `ios-prep`/`main` or family-profile changes.

**Test matrix update:** TYPEWRITER FAIL on original #149 merge (ON and OFF); BALLOONS/FISH ON owner-PASS as reported; CRAWL OFF owner-PASS; CRAWL ON text-only, voice still unsolved. Only after proposed R16b.1 merged: Typewriter ON (keys/alert quiet, spoken voice and transitions normal, no false Welcome Back); Typewriter OFF (normal clicks, narration auto plays, no false Welcome Back), real lock/held App Switcher (correct Welcome Back), quick Balloons/Fish ON and Crawl OFF regression. **Do not infer Keyboard PASS**: still NOT TESTED. Full exact test sequence in [R16b.1 test plan](R16B1-TYPEWRITER-VOICE-HANDOFF.md).

## HISTORICAL R16a.2 proposal before merge/device testing / AUDIO-01 — Learn Chunks/Echo Menu false Welcome Back fix proposed (2026-11-18)

**Owner iPhone evidence and separate issues:** On physical iPhone with Silent ON and **speaker** output, owner reports **narration audible / Home UI pops and Verse Scramble effects quiet — PASS for tested Option B acoustic distinction**. With **AirPods**, all those native optional effects remain audible although debug logs show `AVAudioSessionCategorySoloAmbient` with `BluetoothA2DPOutput`. This is **route-dependent behavior** observed on the device, NOT a stale voice `.playback` lease; do **not** force special Bluetooth muting or claim native Silent-switch detection. Apple’s audio guidance emphasizes the user's choice of output/headphones and silencing nonessential audio; use normal OS category behavior rather than speculative workaround. Flashcards including recorded-voice playback still works in Silent Mode, as owner confirmed. [R16a.1 diagnostic PR #147](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/147) is merged.

**New owner regression:** During **Learn the Verse / Chunks and Echo**, tapping the **Menu** button shows the **Welcome Back** overlay instead of the Learn Menu. The accompanying Xcode transcript shows `BibloZooNativeSound.play` returning `category-not-soloambient` *during* a legitimate spoken `learn-part-...` whose essential-voice lease is temporarily `.playback`; the native effect rejection calls `forgetPlayback()`, emits `biblozooNarration type=invalidated reason=interrupted`, and triggers the JS Learn interruption/Welcome Back path. This is a **false interruption caused by an optional global UI pop**, not proof that the child left UIKit/OS active state.

**R16a.2 proposed patch:** A native sound effect request under the **same currently playing, owner-proven narration `.playback` lease** is still **rejected** (`accepted:false`, `category-not-soloambient`), but it must **not** call `forgetPlayback()` or emit a fake `interrupted` notification. All OTHER blocked cases still use the original fail-closed teardown: genuine UIKit inactive/background, pending OS interruption, media reset, external audio, or a non-owner category mismatch. Swift `BibloZooEssentialVoiceSession.isActive(owner:)` is read-only; no category/mode/volume/microphone change. The real app's existing `openLearnMenu` then owns checkpoint/pause and close-from-gesture resume; no Web fallback, autoplay, reward or profile change.

**Release gate:** R16a.2 remains **NOT DEVICE TESTED** until owner presses Menu during active **Chunks** and **Echo** narration (Silent ON, phone speaker and, if convenient, AirPods) and observes Learn Menu rather than Welcome Back, closes to resume at correct checkpoint, and then holds App Switcher/locks phone to verify genuine OS interruptions STILL use Welcome Back. Also observe unchanged silent-speaker optional effects, Flashcards recording/voice, music/AirPods and rewards; do not treat static Swift source checks as physical proof. Keep R16 overall open pending separate Read My Verse narrator/soundtrack review. No automatic merge, stable `ios-prep` and `main` untouched; no original production-device uninstall, backups or child audio uploads.

**R16a.2 targeted physical steps after PR review/merge:** (1) With Silent ON, speaker route, start Learn > Chunks and tap ☰ **while a spoken part is audibly playing**; the actual Learn Menu must appear immediately, **no** false Welcome Back. Tap Close, verify same part/checkpoint resumes only from the child gesture, not from page transition. (2) Repeat in Echo both narration and child repeat gap. (3) While Learn activity runs, **really hold the App Switcher or lock the iPhone** and come back: existing Welcome Back gate must still occur; no auto replay. (4) Quick Flashcards Record→audible Replay and Home pop silence on phone speaker (already owner-reported PASS before patch; catch regressions only). Mark PASS/FAIL/NOT TESTED with exact installed SHA. No child audio/profile backups/full Xcode logs needed. `node scripts/validate_r16a2_native_menu_optional_sound_guard.js` and existing real Learn Menu N7j VM regression run in CI; neither proves audibility or UIKit behavior.

## CURRENT R14 / PRACTICE-01 — Traffic Tap bonus-truck pacing ([PR #144](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/144))

**R13 / PLAY-02 OWNER PHYSICAL IPHONE PASS / FIXED / CLOSED:** [PR #143](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/143) merged into `integration/native-audio-to-ios-prep` at `113b320733604bede0b1c91f4e0dc77bcbfb268d`. The owner reports **“I merged and tested that PR and it's working great”** and explicitly authorizes marking the Ghost Writer mode-selection popup issue complete. R01–R12b were already accepted, so **R01–R13 now have owner-reported physical-iPhone PASS** for their targeted fixes, not a blanket optional route/iOS-version/device-matrix claim. The installed exact SHA, iOS version and other routes are not supplied. Historical R13 `NOT TESTED` text beneath the *historical R13* heading is pre-acceptance only.

**Owner R14 finding, `PRACTICE-01`:** Traffic Tap's bonus-truck entrance and exit travel **too slowly** as the long front/trailer crosses the field. Source diagnosis: both `startBonusTruckIntro` and `startBonusTruckOutro` in `verse_games/traffic_tap/game.js` used `Math.round(clamp(130 * trafficSpeedMultiplier(), 120, 190))` px/s, where `trafficSpeedMultiplier` scales from Easy (1.0) through Medium (1.3) to Hard (1.45) late in the verse. A ~390px synthetic phone field plus ~520px truck can require ~978px of right-to-left travel; Easy/Normal thus took **~7.5s by source math** (not a measured physical device transit). This truck also ignored the existing `VerseGameShell.getGameSpeedMultiplier()` Normal 1×, Fast 1.25× and Max 1.5× speed presets, unlike ordinary traffic.

**Focused [R14 PR #144](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/144), `fix/r14-traffic-tap-bonus-truck-pacing` → integration only:** Dedicated pure `bonusTruckTravelSpeed()` uses `Math.round(clamp(300 * trafficSpeedMultiplier() * VerseGameShell.getGameSpeedMultiplier(), 280, 560))` px/s, identical for intro and outro. Easy/Normal becomes **300 px/s**, Fast **375** and Max **450**, at least ~2.3× the previous Easy speed; higher medium/hard values safely cap at 560. Captured once at the start of each phase; no mid-crossing speed shifts. The original start `fieldWidth + 28`, right-half lane-clear requirement, per-frame `x -= speed * (dt / 1000)`, **34ms active-frame clamp**, `x < -(bonusTruckWidth + 40)` full-trailer exit gates and all original transitions stay untouched. Intro still starts the actual timed/scored bonus round **only after full exit**; outro shows results **only after full exit**. A 560px/s cap plus 34ms dt means no more than ~19px of movement per frame. **Ordinary vehicles**, streak/traffic acceleration, collisions, bonus target/score, rainbow bus, round timing, sprites, truck intro/outro music/volume, muting, audio files and native session stay unchanged. This is a visual timing/feel change only; phone/tablet rendering and readability still need owner acceptance.

**Regression and real-device gate:** New `scripts/validate_r14_traffic_tap_bonus_truck_pacing.js`, wired to `.github/workflows/ios-prep-validation.yml`, executes **the real production Traffic Tap speed function, truck intro/outro, main/bonus phase branches, activity-time loop and pause** with synthetic geometry. Nine combinations of Easy/Medium/Hard × Normal/Fast/Max, correct scale/caps, original right-lane clearance, one original intro/outro cue, exact offscreen gates before reward/start/end, menu/Welcome Back pause freeze and no resume teleport, narrower phone/wider tablet. Existing N6l native Traffic Tap PCM/UI/PiP, R01–R13 regressions, Swift parser and production Web build continue. **R14 owner physical iPhone: NOT TESTED.** CI synthetic geometry is not measured iPhone animation smoothness or perceived pacing. On actual phone: play Easy/Normal to bonus transition and exit, then Fast/Max; truck should cross **noticeably faster but readable**, never clip/skip its full trailer, duplicate score/reward, change ordinary vehicle speeds, skip Bonus or prematurely render results. Test Medium/Hard if desired; game speed picker prior to bonus, Menu/held App Switcher/Apple Music/AirPods freezing and genuine Welcome Back, original sounds, target visibility, countdown/rainbow bus, pause/resume and ordinary gameplay. Report whether 300px/s looks right or needs a small R14b feel adjustment. No tests or claims made on owner's actual device by automation.

**Safety/next:** Original production `com.andydoane.biblozoo` holds **10 private irreplaceable Zookeeper profiles**; separate Audio Test `com.andydoane.biblozoo.audiotest` has **11 DIFFERENT profiles** and backups are not interchangeable. No uninstall/reset/restore/import, child audio/PCM/profile/backup upload, or production-ID install without separate owner approval and correct verified off-device production backup. PR remains open for owner review/merge and physical test, stable `ios-prep` and `main` untouched. **R15 `AUDIO-01` Silent Mode is an owner product-policy decision PR, NOT automatic implementation or an unapproved `.soloAmbient` category change**; consider it only after R14 owner acceptance.

**R14 physical-iPhone review checklist (PASS / FAIL / NOT TESTED for observed cases only):**

1. Owner reviews and merges [PR #144](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/144) to the integration branch. An original production-bundle install still needs **separate approval** and off-device backup of the **10 production Zookeepers**, not the unrelated **11 Audio Test profiles**. Never uninstall/reset/import/restore/upload child data.
2. Go to **Practice Games → Traffic Tap**, choose **Easy / Normal** speed, finish the main verse sequence. Watch the bonus truck enter from the right **only once the correct upper lane is clear**. The long vehicle must travel at a **brisk but legible** pace, its bonus target should be visible while it passes, and the Bonus round must start **only after the full trailer has left the left edge**. No strange collision, skipped target, premature start or stuck transition.
3. Play the timed Bonus normally; check taps, score, rainbow bus and timer are ordinary, and the truck **outro** also travels briskly and fully off-screen **before** the real Results/Complete screen appears, exactly once. Audio cue for each transition should still play once; no new volume change or duplicate sound.
4. On another run, select **Fast** and optionally **Max** in the **Speed** menu before the bonus. The truck should cross faster, consistent with the selected preset and difficulty (optionally try Medium/Hard), without teleporting or disappearing before the full vehicle clears. Ordinary traffic cars, spawn spacing, speed menu, streak rewards, touch hitbox and score must not change.
5. While the truck is entering or exiting, open **Menu**, hold **App Switcher** for ~10–15 seconds, or let Apple Music/AirPods/PiP interrupt if available. The truck and animation/game clock must **freeze** until the existing deliberate resume/Welcome Back, then continue at the same x-position without a huge jump, skipped bonus/reward or native output autoplay. Check sound gating and mute remain unchanged.
6. If it still feels too slow or becomes too quick, tell us the speed preset/difficulty and optionally the approximate perceived crossing time/feel—only your iPhone can validate that product choice. The automated test uses synthetic geometry and cannot conclude a physical PASS.

**R14 close gate:** owner confirms the bonus-truck entry and exit feel appropriately brisk, stay visually readable and preserve full-trailer exit, round start/results, ordinary traffic, pause and audio. Until then **R14 is an unmerged source/test candidate, NOT iPhone PASS**.

## HISTORICAL R13 / PLAY-02 — Ghost Writer mode-selection transient popup ([PR #143](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/143))

**R12b / READ-01 owner physical iPhone PASS / FIXED / CLOSED:** [PR #142](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/142) merged into `integration/native-audio-to-ios-prep` at `b4a85d1a213d7d4c4e501d700bf07b607b74c7c3`. The owner subsequently says **“I tested that PR and it worked.”** This resolves the verse-dependent **three-line vs two-line Typewriter intro** from the first R12 #141 iPhone test, which the owner had called **“99% working”** before R12b. **Close R12/READ-01** as owner-reported accepted with targeted physical iPhone follow-up; retain R12 original quote/source tests, Typewriter/Keyboard intro regression and all R01–R11 passes. Exact installed source SHA, iOS version and unreported optional hardware/VoiceOver routes not provided. Any earlier R12b **“NOT TESTED”** statements below the *historical* heading are pre-acceptance notes.

**R13 original owner finding `PLAY-02`:** Ghost Writer briefly flashes an unexpected popup/screen immediately after selecting a mode, then proceeds normally. The owner did **not** supply a frame capture or the exact text of that popup. We must not pretend its visual identity has been verified. The source contains a highly specific likely mechanism on native iOS: `renderModeSelect` calls `primeNativeGhostFromGesture(() => startRun(mode), "start")`; this unconditionally appended a **full-screen `#ghostNativePreparing`** panel (“Getting spooky sounds ready…”) while seven native PCM files were prepared and removed it as soon as the Promise resolved. Fast resolution can visibly paint a transient status for a frame or two. Neither the actual training “Draw each letter nice and big!” message nor Photos permission/export dialogs are evidence of a defect, and they must not be removed. The brief popup's precise identity still requires owner-device confirmation.

**Focused [R13 PR #143](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/143), branch `fix/r13-ghost-writer-mode-transition-preparing-flash` → integration only:** Delay **only the visible `#ghostNativePreparing` layer by 350ms**, using a single timer fenced by the existing native preflight generation, current pending state and `document.hidden`. Start **native `prepareFromGesture(uiTap)` synchronously on the original child mode tap** exactly as before; successful fast bank prep transitions straight to the real Training screen without a useless loading popup. If work takes longer, retain the original accessible “Getting spooky sounds ready…” overlay and Back to Playground; a denied prepare still shows the existing **Sound isn't ready / Try Again** modal with an explicit new gesture, and temporary Apple Music/PiP interruptions still use Welcome Back. Cancel the pending timer and remove temporary loading on success, actual failure, fresh retry, loading Back and native UIKit inactive even when WebKit remains visible. While the loading overlay is deferred the mode-select Back remains accessible: tapping it explicitly increments the attempt generation, clears pending action/dialog and calls native stop so **a late Promise can never unexpectedly open Training**. Duplicate mode taps are still blocked. The action/handwriting, original seven PCM bank, `.soloAmbient`, spoken effects, muted/Web/PWA output policy, external app takeover, gesture-only ownership, native route and app lifecycle are unchanged. No changes to shared shell/Swift, Ghost Writer training 1.5-second instruction, drawing, Remix, Photos/Files picker, export/permission UI, other Playground games or stored profiles.

**Automation and owner gate:** New `scripts/validate_r13_ghost_writer_mode_preparing_flash.js` in CI executes the **actual production Ghost Writer mode-select + native dialog/preflight code** against a controlled fake DOM, Promise and timer: quick success never inserts loading; truly slow work does; pending double-tap does not double-start; denial retains real Try Again; temporary external audio yields Welcome Back; Back before/after loading fences stale success; native inactivity cancels even while WebKit remains visible; warm native PCM still activates from real gesture. Existing N6r/R01–R12b, Swift parse, native-only audio isolation, export/picker and production Web build are preserved. **R13 physical iPhone: NOT TESTED.** This source candidate does not confirm the unidentified owner flash until actual visual testing. On iPhone select **Beginner and Advanced** repeatedly from Ghost Writer Mode Select; the quick success must go directly to ordinary Training with **no flickering full-screen progress**. Slow native preparation may still legitimately show a stable progress panel; real denial/Retry/Apple Music/AirPods/PiP remains safe and must not be suppressed. Check mode Back during pending, 10–15s held App Switcher, normal Training's intentional “Draw each letter nice and big!”, drawing/Next, Photo picker/export and other games. If flash persists, seek a **short non-private** video/frame description; diagnose the precise overlay rather than broad CSS hiding. Don't claim real-device PASS from CI. No auto-merge or stable `ios-prep`/`main` promotion.

**Privacy/deployment gate:** Production `com.andydoane.biblozoo` retains **10 irreplaceable private Zookeeper profiles**; separate `com.andydoane.biblozoo.audiotest` contains **11 different profiles**. Never conflate their off-device backups, uninstall/reset/restore/import or upload child's voice/PCM/profile/backup. Production-ID app update needs separately explicit owner approval and verified correct production backup. R14 `PRACTICE-01` Traffic Tap bonus-truck pacing follows **only after R13 owner acceptance**.

**R13 on-device physical iPhone checklist — mark PASS / FAIL / NOT TESTED only for routes actually tried:**

1. Review and merge [PR #143](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/143) to `integration/native-audio-to-ios-prep`; any original production-ID iPhone install separately requires explicit owner approval and correct off-device backup for its **10 original profiles** (isolated Audio Test app has **11 different profiles**). Do not uninstall/reset/import/restore or upload any private child data.
2. In **Playground → Ghost Writer → Start → Choose Your Ghost**, select **Beginner** and **Advanced** several times. On a fast successful selection expect **no full-screen “Getting spooky sounds ready…” flash** between the mode buttons and the real Training canvas. Preserve original mode-specific letter count and guide. The intentional **“Draw each letter nice and big!”** first-training message lasts ~1.5s and is not the target; it should still show and fade normally.
3. If iOS native audio preparation genuinely takes longer than 350ms, the original progress overlay may appear normally **with Back to Playground**; it should not flash unnecessarily for quick success. If native output fails, the existing genuine **Sound isn't ready / Try Again** must remain visible and retry only after a new child tap. Do not intentionally cause mic/profile damage to force an error.
4. Try rapid repeated mode taps, a quick **Back to Ghost Writer title** while preparation is still pending, and the original loading Back if it appears: **no late Training screen** should pop up after Back. Confirm Back to Playground never loses the owner app. Check Apple Music/AirPods/PiP/10–15-second held App Switcher if you use them: a genuine interruption shows one normal Welcome Back; no late loading popup, no native autoplay, original UI taps/spooky effects resume only after gesture.
5. Check training drawing, Clear/Undo/Next, the intentional intro and guide fade, Remix/playback, native Photos/Files chooser and save video/picture modals; those are **real dialogs**, not candidate artifacts to hide. Smoke-test other Playground games and normal Web/PWA Ghost Writer UI. If an unexpected flash remains, capture only **non-private** frames or describe the text; do not include children's profile, handwriting, voice, photo or data in GitHub/ChatGPT.

**Acceptance decision:** Owner-reported repeatable no-flicker with unchanged legitimate preparation/error/permission UI and audio/gameplay on physical iPhone = R13 PASS. CI alone or absence of popup in a fake DOM = **NOT TESTED** on device.

## HISTORICAL R12b / READ-01 follow-up — fixed two-line Read typing intro ([PR #142](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/142))

**Owner post-merge physical-iPhone R12 finding — NEAR-PASS / FOLLOW-UP NEEDED, NOT FULLY CLOSED:** [R12 PR #141](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/141) merged to `integration/native-audio-to-ios-prep` at `686ceba9825eae342c416df113f3a30c7920f4d4`. After testing R12 on a real iPhone, owner reports it **“99% working”**: original quotation-mark grouping appears to have worked well overall, but the **Typewriter launch instruction varies its line breaks by selected verse**. For **Acts 5:29**, they saw **three lines: “Tap to” / “type” / “the verse.”**; for **Acts 16:31**, **two lines: “Tap to type” / “the verse.”** They request **consistently two lines**, sized to fill the available horizontal width and independent of the verse. This is new owner-reported device evidence, **not** R12 full PASS or proof of comprehensive R12 mode coverage. R01–R11 remain separately owner-reported physical iPhone PASS.

**Source diagnosis:** In `read_my_verse.js`, `restoreReadTypingFit` and `fitReadTypingVerseText` set `--typewriter-intro-font-size` from the **verse's own** computed/cached `fontSize`. Long verses shrink the intro, short verses enlarge it. The old intro markup only kept “the verse.” together with a `nowrap` tail; it never guaranteed the first phrase “Tap to type” remains one line. This is a deterministic source-level explanation of the observed 3-line versus 2-line wrapping, not a native/iPhone audio problem.

**Focused R12b [PR #142](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/142), branch `fix/r12b-read-typing-intro-two-line-independent-fit` → integration ONLY:** Replace the dynamic-wrap intro with exactly **two nonbreaking lines** using the original characters `Tap to type ` and `the verse.` in `renderTypingIntroText`. Keep the hidden full-sentence measuring guide + hidden yet-untyped letters, overlayed real typed characters, 75ms/letter sound/animation, 3s hold, 300ms fade, accessibility, reduced-motion rules, and first-tap gating. Remove **both verse-fit-to-intro size assignments**, leaving the existing per-verse font sizing, cache, chunk display, R12 quote grouping and source/audio indices untouched. New `fitReadTypingIntro(wrap)` measures the first and second intro guide lines in the **current screen's intro box** using the actual loaded font, fits to approximately 98% of available horizontal width while respecting two-line height, and writes only the intro-specific font variable. Runs on initial/delayed layout, font readiness and rotation. Keyboard shares the same fixed-layout geometry and retains its green font treatment; it is tested independently rather than assumed from Typewriter. Responsive fallback before first measurement; no content/verse ID input to the intro fitter.

**Regression and physical gate:** `scripts/validate_r12b_read_typing_intro_independent_fit.js` executes the **actual production JS intro renderer and intro geometry fitter** with synthetic Typewriter/Keyboard stage widths and heights, and validates no verse-size coupling, identical fitted size at identical dimensions across synthetic Acts 5:29/16:31, fully reserved two-line text throughout animation, reasonable width fill/no overflow, rotation/font hooks, and unchanged timing. Existing `scripts/validate_read_my_verse.js` asserts independent intro sizing; CI retains all R01–R12/N7 audio recovery and production Web build. **R12b iPhone NOT TESTED.** Real iPhone WebKit font metrics, landscape and screenshots still require owner's physical observation. Verify Acts 5:29 and Acts 16:31 Typewriter show **two lines “Tap to type” / “the verse.”** at the same comfortable width, with no third line or clipping; check Keyboard, portrait/rotation, ghost-letter positioning, original per-verse text layout and R12 opening-quote/typed B/audio chunk mapping after intro. No AVAudioSession/category, Apple Music, AirPods, Welcome Back, Flashcards, other games, profile or private voice changes. Stable `ios-prep`/`main` unmodified; no automated merge/promote. Original production `com.andydoane.biblozoo` has **10 private Zookeepers**; separate Audio Test `.audiotest` has **11 different profiles**. Maintain *separate* off-device private backups, require explicit owner approval before any production-ID install, never uninstall/reset/import/restore/upload voice or profiles. **R13 `PLAY-02` Ghost Writer picker flash stays a separate follow-up, not bundled.**

**R12b owner physical iPhone acceptance checklist — PASS / FAIL / NOT TESTED:**

1. After owner review/merge and separately approved original production-bundle install **with the correct 10-profile off-device private backup** (not isolated Audio Test's 11 distinct profiles), open the normal **Review → Learn → Listen Activities → Typewriter** picker. Don't uninstall/reset/restore/import; no voice/profile/backup upload.
2. Open **Acts 5:29** and **Acts 16:31** on the same iPhone in the same orientation. During the one-time typing introduction, both must read **line 1 “Tap to type” / line 2 “the verse.”** with *exactly two visible lines*. The first line should fill the useful horizontal width without touching/clipping screen edges. Neither line should shift, wrap or resize as letters type. Compare at normal and reduced-motion accessibility settings if used.
3. Repeat in **Keyboard**: terminal-green intro uses the same two lines and its own fitted screen box; do not change the original Keyboard verse styling. Optionally rotate to landscape or switch to a different screen width: introductory text may scale with viewport but **not** with the selected Bible verse. Check no clipping on short display height.
4. Let both intros finish and fade as usual: the scripture body should still have its **own independent variable font size** for each verse. A real tap still reveals one letter, original audio feedback/intro keystrokes and ~75ms cadence remain. No skipped tap or stuck introduction.
5. For Acts 16:31, revisit original R12 quotation mark: first typed chunk ends at **“They replied,”**; next starts with the opening quote plus **Believe**, with original `a`/`b` narration files, letter count and progression. If tested, check held App Switcher/Apple Music/AirPods interruptions still require real Welcome Back and no stale audio. Smoke-test other Read modes and Learn/Flashcards; R12b is presentation only.
6. Report the verses/modes/orientations actually tested, and whether the **two fixed lines are stable**. **R12 / READ-01 remains OPEN pending owner acceptance of the merged R12b follow-up; R13 Ghost Writer stays separate.**

## HISTORICAL R12 / READ-01 — opening quotation marks stay with next typed phrase ([PR #141](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/141))

**R11 / LEARN-01 owner physical-iPhone PASS / FIXED / CLOSED:** [PR #140](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/140) merged into `integration/native-audio-to-ios-prep` at `97d31507b22e92f541ec800e10b08801695ab22e`. The owner reports a real iPhone test and states it is **“doing everything you said it would.”** Mark the extra Learn Welcome Back busy-message bug **closed** after R01–R10 were already accepted. The exact iPhone installed source SHA, iOS version and optional route coverage were not provided; do not claim additional scenarios passed. Earlier R11 `NOT TESTED` text under the *historical R11 heading* below describes pre-acceptance status only.

**Original R12 / `READ-01` owner observation:** In Read My Verse **Typewriter** and possibly **Keyboard**, the spoken first chunk is **“They replied”**, but the first displayed/typed chunk finishes **“They replied, ‘”**, incorrectly leaving the *opening* quotation mark with words that precede the quoted speech. The canonical example is **“They replied, ‘Believe in the Lord Jesus…’”**. This is distinct from the obsolete pre-picker `READ-ACCESS` testing obstacle, already resolved by PR #124. No need to reopen a child-facing DEV tester.

**Source-supported cause:** `read_my_verse.js` has `buildDisplayChunks(verseText, echoParts)`, which locates each spoken `echoParts` phrase inside canonical Scripture and forms each display chunk from one phrase start **through the next phrase start**. The punctuation and whitespace preceding the next phrase are therefore included at the end of the previous *visual* chunk, including an opening quote. The original `echoParts` narration index and the actual audio content are not wrong; the mismatch is display-only. Do not alter Scripture data, recorded speech, MP3 file paths or native audio ownership to address it.

**Focused [R12 PR #141](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/141), `fix/r12-read-typing-opening-quote-boundary` → integration only:** The original `buildDisplayChunks` stays **unchanged** for all activities. For **Typewriter and Keyboard only**, if the ordinary mapped chunks reconstruct the **exact canonical `verseText`**, `groupTypingOpeningQuotes` shifts an unambiguously opening curly quote (`‘`, `“`, etc.) or a **contextually opening** straight ASCII quote (`'`, `"`) plus its existing adjacent whitespace from the preceding visual chunk to the beginning of the next typed phrase. It leaves commas, closing curly quotes, word-ending straight apostrophes, contractions, unquoted boundaries and already-correct `echoParts` untouched. It neither adds nor discards one character: `chunks.join("") === verseText`. The quote is visible at the start of the *next* typed chunk via existing `getInitialRevealCount`; the first child tap still types the next **letter**, without requiring a punctuation tap. If `echoParts` fails to map to canonical Scripture, leave the older fallback alone instead of inventing a boundary. **All original numbered narration indices `a`, `b`, ... remain the same**; R12 does not modify chunk completion clocks, iPhone interruption recovery, audio files or scoring. The DEV forced-short/long mode still chooses its original narrated segment based on unmodified chunk lengths. **Tap Words in Order, Unscramble, Crawl, Balloons, Fish and Chunk Sequence are not changed** by this visual grouping.

**Automated and physical acceptance:** New `scripts/validate_r12_read_typing_opening_quotes.js` is wired into `.github/workflows/ios-prep-validation.yml` and runs the **actual production Read My Verse module** on synthetic examples covering original `They replied, ‘Believe...`, curly/straight single and double quotes, paired closers, final apostrophe, contraction, nested quotes, unmatched fallback and no-quote text. Tests validate canonical joined text, unchanged `audioIndices` / `getChunkAudioPath` suffixes, first-letter tap / no extra punctuation tap, both Typewriter and Keyboard, unchanged non-typing activity chunks and original forced-short/long test-mode selection. Preserve N7/R01–R11 and production Web build. **R12 owner physical iPhone: NOT TESTED.** Source/unit tests do not prove actual iPhone rendering or audio sync. [TESTING.md](TESTING.md) covers Review → Learn → Listen Activities → Typewriter and Keyboard, first/next chunk visual boundaries, exact verse, correct spoken `a`/`b` order, punctuation-only/quote cases, interruption checkpoint and no other Read regressions. Original production `com.andydoane.biblozoo` has **10 private Zookeepers** and separate Audio Test bundle **11 different profiles**; protect independently backed up private data. No uninstall/reset/restore/import or profile/voice uploads; production-ID device install requires separate owner authorization. No auto-merge or stable `ios-prep`/`main` promotion. **R13 `PLAY-02`** is next only after owner acceptance of R12.

**R12 focused physical-iPhone acceptance checklist — report PASS / FAIL / NOT TESTED for the tested cases:**

1. Review and merge [PR #141](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/141) to integration only. If updating the original `com.andydoane.biblozoo` production-ID app, require **separate owner authorization** and an off-device **10-Zookeeper private production backup**; the isolated Audio Test app's **11 different profiles** are not interchangeable. Do not uninstall/reset/import/restore or share voices/private JSON.

2. Via **Review → Learn → Listen Activities**, open **Typewriter** on a verse with the passage **“They replied, ‘Believe in the Lord Jesus…”** (or another with an opening quote between spoken chunks). The first spoken/typed chunk should finish after **“They replied,”**; the opening quote should appear with the *next* visually typed chunk **“‘Believe…”**. The full verse, spacing, comma and quotes should be identical to Scripture, with no orphan/doubled/disappearing quote.

3. On the next chunk, the opening quote should be **already visible**; the child's first typing tap should reveal **B** (the first actual letter), not require tapping once solely for the quote. Continue through the chunk and verify normal sound, word feedback, next-chunk progression and success only after genuine completion. **Keyboard** must do the same. If available, try straight `'`/`"`, curly `‘`/`“`, and a closing quote or contraction to ensure closers/apostrophes stay where they belong.

4. Compare narration: **original spoken chunk a** (“They replied”) still plays for displayed chunk 1, and **original chunk b** (“Believe in the Lord Jesus…”) plays for displayed chunk 2. No skipped or repeated audio, moved file index, false completion, or altered mute. Optionally test the full-verse and forced-short/long DEV lab controls only in the separate DEV bundle if safely available.

5. After holding App Switcher or allowing AirPods/Apple Music to interrupt Typewriter/Keyboard, use the existing **Welcome Back** and a real explicit resume gesture. The same chunk and character position should return, with original R07/R08 cue timing and no auto audio restart, ghost animation or changed verse. Spot-check **Tap in Order**, **Unscramble**, **Balloons/Fish/Crawl**, Flashcards voice and other Welcome Back UI; their punctuation/audio should be unaffected.

**Closure:** R12 remains OPEN until the owner confirms physically on iPhone that **both** Typewriter and Keyboard display the opening quote with the subsequent phrase while keeping the original spoken chunk order. CI cannot establish real-device pass.

## HISTORICAL R11 / LEARN-01 — duplicate Learn Welcome Back busy status (PR #140)

**R10 / READ-04 OWNER PHYSICAL IPHONE PASS / FIXED / CLOSED:** [PR #139](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/139) was merged into integration at `6fbeb125c792a46845e2ab9a4992827bd53c08a4`. Shared `BibloZooWelcomeBack.setAction({busy:true})` already renders rotating `[data-welcome-back-progress]` text in the reserved action slot with its own `role=status`/`aria-live=polite` and `aria-busy` state; Learn's separate `syncLearnAudioResumeOverlay()` in `app.js` then mirrors `getMessage(overlay)` into `#learnAudioResumeStatus`. Two independently live paragraphs announce the same progress. Source proves this UI duplication mechanism, not that audio/narration recovery itself is broken.

**[R11 PR #140](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/140), `fix/r11-learn-welcome-back-duplicate-busy-status` → integration only:** While a Learn recovery attempt is busy and the shared progress slot exists, keep Learn's separate status paragraph **empty and hidden**. A genuine failed narration verification still shows **“Sound isn't ready yet. Please try again.”** under an enabled **Try again!** button. A new tap clears the stale error. If shared markup is unavailable, the local fallback displays only a simple **“Checking sound...”** status while its button is disabled. The shared Welcome Back helper/CSS, Zoo Escape, other overlays, audio session, Apple Music/AirPods takeover, Learn Menu/HIDE active-time clocks, mute, Read/Flashcards/Feeding, Web audio policy, scores and profiles remain unchanged.

**Verification and owner gate:** `scripts/validate_r11_learn_welcome_back_busy_status.js` runs the **actual production Learn sync function** and **actual shared Welcome Back helper** in a synthetic DOM, covering initial action, single rotating accessible busy phrase, idempotent timer, disabled action, genuine error and retry, stale-error clearing, verified success hiding, menu and local fallback. CI also retains N7/R01–R10, Swift grammar and production Web build. Synthetic DOM checks cannot certify iPhone WebKit, VoiceOver, physical AirPods or real narration. **R11 OWNER PHYSICAL IPHONE: NOT TESTED.** [TESTING.md](TESTING.md) asks owner to compare Learn Listen/Chunks/Echo recovery on iPhone with Apple Music/AirPods/held switcher: one playful phrase only, real audio verification and no early dismissal/auto-play, readable true error/Retry after refusal, no other Welcome Back changes. No production-ID device install without separate owner approval and private off-device backup. The original app has **10 private Zookeepers**, separate Audio Test has **11 different profiles**. Never uninstall/reset/restore/import/upload child voice/private JSON. Stable `ios-prep` and `main` remain unpromoted. **Next R12 `READ-01`** quote/chunk grouping after R11 acceptance.

**Focused physical iPhone R11 checklist — report PASS/FAIL/NOT TESTED:**

1. After reviewing/merging PR #140 and separately authorizing any production-ID update with the correct private original 10-Zookeeper off-device backup, use the usual GitHub Desktop Pull Origin → npm build → Capacitor sync → Xcode iPhone workflow. Never uninstall/reset/import/restore or upload private voices, profile JSON or app backup.
2. Learn **Listen to the Verse**: interrupt with AirPods/Apple Music external Play or held App Switcher, return to **Welcome Back**, tap **Let's go!** once. The rotating “Cleaning the zoo...”, “Tickling the giraffes...” etc. should appear ONLY in the black button-shaped action slot, NOT again underneath. Only one live progress status should be announced by VoiceOver where accessible.
3. Real narration progress still dismisses the overlay only after verification; no first-tap loss, fake reward, automatic audio takeover, duplicate overlay or active lesson timer advancement. If the output is legitimately refused or verification stalls, **Try again!** remains clickable with the separate error explanation beneath it.
