5. Report only the routes actually tested and whether you saw any duplicate phrase or stale error. R11 is not closed on CI alone.

## HISTORICAL R10 / READ-04 — relative native Read word/melody volume ([PR #139](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/139))

**R09b / FLASH-05 owner physical-iPhone PASS / FIXED / CLOSED:** [PR #138](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/138) was merged into `integration/native-audio-to-ios-prep` at `9abff454e34e2068f6051f09b8958c67731d72e4`. Owner **merged, physically tested** R09b and reports recorded-voice playback volume is **pretty good**, explicitly authorizing us to **mark R09/FLASH-05 complete**. This closes R09 and its earlier insufficient #137 candidate; it does **not** mean the original #137 alone was accepted. R01–R08 were already owner-reported iPhone PASS. The exact locally installed SHA, iOS version and unreported optional playback routes were not supplied; do not infer full-matrix testing. Earlier R09b `NOT TESTED` paragraphs below are **historical**, not current status.

**R10 owner symptom, not calibrated audio measurement:** `READ-04` says **Tap in Order** and **Unscramble** word beeps and little melodies sounded quieter than other games' Practice/Playground feedback at similar device volume. Their native output is `BibloZooNativeReadOutputPlugin` in `ios/App/App/SceneDelegate.swift`, not `BibloZooNativeSoundPlugin.swift`. The native fixed `wavData` voices used **0.22 peak gain** per correct note, **0.11 wrong**, **0.08 optional negative**, while some other native game sound recipes generate stronger peaks or mix more notes. For example, Tower of Bible correct tone's 0.07 authored per-note amplitude uses a 3.40 source multiplier in its independent native PCM synthesizer. This is a source-supported **relative-gain hypothesis**, not a speaker/AirPods loudness or perceived-dB measurement. Web Read also has an authored 0.22 peak and keeps it unchanged. Do not raise every app sound or change hardware volume based on this observation.

**Focused R10 [PR #139](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/139), branch `fix/r10-read-native-cue-loudness-balance` → integration only:** Increase native Read **correct single-word** order/scramble feedback **0.22 → 0.32** (roughly +3.3 dB source amplitude). Set **each note of a four-note completion** to a gentler **0.28**, since completion voices overlap; set wrong Order feedback to **0.13** (previous 0.11, still softer), and keep optional native negative at **0.08**. DEV-only waveform Lab audition aligns with child volumes (**0.32 single, 0.28 completion**) and keeps matched gain between waveforms. Existing native 44.1 kHz mono 16-bit synthesis, original scale/pitches, individual 0.17/0.2s note lengths, attack/release, precise **0/90/100/180/190/270/300ms** authored note spacing, R08 immediate-first/live-shared-clock scheduling, delegate completion and 12-voice cap stay intact. No Read narration, Crawl music, generated typing-key sounds, Web/PWA/Android gains, other games, Flashcards mic/leveling, mute/Start gesture policy, AVAudioSession, Apple Music/AirPods/held-switcher recovery, score or profile changes.

**Automation and owner gate:** New `scripts/validate_r10_read_native_cue_loudness.js`, wired into `.github/workflows/ios-prep-validation.yml`, compiles and executes the **actual Swift native Read WAV synthesis function** with synthetic samples. It verifies the source waveform's 0.32 / 0.28 / 0.13 / 0.08 peaks, correct vs previous 0.22 source RMS, and bounded two-/four-note overlap below 0.66 amplitude on synthetic mixes; asserts unchanged authored timing, R08 cancel/readiness, DEV comparison and Web original authoring. The existing R08 and N7f2 validators are updated **only for expected deliberately changed native gains** and continue to enforce source clock/timbre/safety constraints. The rest of N7/R01–R09 and production Web build remain required. These Swift/Linux tests are **not** Apple SDK linking, acoustic measurements or proof of what a child hears. **R10 physical iPhone: NOT TESTED.** Review and separately approve any production-ID device install after private backup. Compare **five correct Tap in Order taps, wrong taps, completion flourish, two-note Unscramble word confirmation, four-note finish**, at fixed iPhone system volume with Practice/Playground beeps and original Read narration on **speaker and AirPods**; check no harsh/distorted or startlingly loud reward, no silent first tap after Apple Music/held App Switcher + real Welcome Back, no compressed/rushed melody, ghost notes, skipped scoring or mute regression. Preserve separate original production **10-Zookeeper** backup vs **11-profile Audio Test** backup; no uninstall/reset/restore/import or voice/profile upload. Owner must report R10 PASS/FAIL separately before closing; don't automatically promote to stable `ios-prep` or `main`. **Next separate roadmap item: R11 `LEARN-01` duplicate Welcome Back status.**

**Focused R10 owner physical-iPhone checklist (PASS / FAIL / NOT TESTED):**

1. After owner PR review/merge, use only the authorized iPhone integration build. If installation uses original `com.andydoane.biblozoo`, require separate approval and an off-device backup of the original **10 private Zookeepers** (not the separate `.audiotest` app's **11 different profiles**). Never uninstall/reset/import or upload private child audio/records for this test.
2. Set an ordinary comfortable device volume. On **iPhone speaker**, enter Read My Verse **Tap in Order**. Tap **five successive correct words**: each note should be audible and a little closer to typical Practice/Playground beeps; the **four-note finish** should still be gentle, spaced, and not much louder than narration. Try one wrong tap: noticeably softer than correct. Optionally try a generated negative feedback cue: still gentle. Do not change the iOS system volume within this A/B.
3. Test **Unscramble** two-note word confirmation (distinct ~100ms note spacing) and the separated four-note completion at original ~0/90/190/300ms offsets. Listen for clicking/clipping, harshness, overstated reward stingers, sudden unintended loudness or doubling. Compare with a familiar Practice/Playground tone at the same hardware volume. Web/Safari is unaffected.
4. Repeat 2–3 with **AirPods** at a comfortable matched route setting. After playing Apple Music on the AirPods for **10–15s** or holding the App Switcher/lock, return and acknowledge **Welcome Back** via a real gesture. The **very first** correct word must sound immediately; next taps and melody notes must not bunch up or play late. Interruption and mute must still prevent stale cues. No silent immediate feedback, auto audio takeover, double reward or invented success.
5. Quick R09b Flashcards dry recording and meter check, R07 Read Balloons/Fish, R08 Read first-note/melody clocks, Read instructions and Crawl music, Home/Practice pops; check original profiles/scoring remain intact. R10 only adjusts native Read `order`/`scramble` and wrong feedback, not browser, other native game levels or system volume.

**Decision:** Report relative volume and sound quality on speaker/AirPods and which interruption routes were actually tried. Source + CI support a cautious change, **not calibrated acoustic parity**. Mark R10 closed only after owner physical-iPhone acceptance.

## HISTORICAL R09b / FLASH-05 — audible native dry voice and responsive 16-bar mic meter ([PR #138](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/138))

**New physical-iPhone owner result for the merged R09 [#137](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/137): FOLLOW-UP NEEDED / AUDIBILITY GOAL NOT MET.** R09 #137 merged into `integration/native-audio-to-ios-prep` at `829e8f1f97e00cb07b23b630fa735ac1e5130705`. Owner reported that **even speaking quite loudly, native Flashcards recorded-voice playback only just about matched the narrated instructions**; while speaking loudly, the 16-bar recording meter rose only to **about 50%**. This is a real-device observation, not a calibrated dB comparison, a broken microphone finding, a Wacky effect regression, or owner acceptance of R09. **FLASH-05 remains OPEN.** All R01–R08 are previously owner-reported physical-iPhone PASS; do not change their acceptance.

**Source-based explanation:** Native Flashcards records raw mono RAM-only Float32 PCM under `AVAudioSession.playAndRecord/.measurement`, while Web uses browser MediaRecorder and instruction prompts are fixed prerecorded MP3s. R09 #137 applied at most 1.8× dry makeup and could refuse a whole quiet take due to a single sharp peak. The old native live RMS-to-meter formula `(dBFS + 55)/55` shows **50% at roughly -27.5 dBFS RMS**; it is not a clipping meter and its 50% does not mean sound is being recorded at half amplitude. The precise on-device mic and playback level differences are unknown; do not assert broken hardware, require the meter reach 100%, or change mic AGC/.measurement mode/audio-session policies based on the bar display.

**Focused [R09b PR #138](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/138), branch `fix/r09b-flashcards-voice-leveling-and-meter` → integration ONLY, iPhone NOT TESTED:** On the existing capture worker, retain only full-take RMS/peak coarse numeric read-only diagnostics and a measured **1.0–3.2×** maximum dry voice makeup factor (up to ~10 dB) when there is enough active speech and no near-silent/noisy/already-loud source. One high consonant no longer vetoes a quiet take. During the **child's explicit normal Play My Recording gesture**, apply a **~12ms attack / 180ms release voice activity envelope**, blend boost back down across silent gaps, and apply **smooth approximately 2:1 peak compression above 0.22** with ~80ms gain-release and a final **0.84 full-scale peak ceiling** in a **temporary dry-only output PCM buffer**. This is intentionally mild and may still need a real-device refinement; it does not make a calibrated loudness claim. The original saved RAM take remains byte-for-byte unchanged (including repeated Play), and all original Wacky/chorus/DEV effect presets still process original PCM. The microphone capture input, countdown, `.measurement` capture mode, `.soloAmbient` output policy, AVAudioSession priority and interruption/route lifecycle, child UI progression, Web/PWA/Android, voice privacy, profile stores and scoring are untouched. For native ONLY, rescale display-only live 16-bar RMS meter to `(dBFS+50)/35`: the old 50% input shows **~64% (10 of 16 bars)** and the upper bars respond more naturally without faking volume or changing PCM.

**Automated and physical gate:** Existing `scripts/validate_r09_flashcards_measured_dry_level.js` is updated to compile and execute the **actual production Swift level-estimator and normal dry render/copy code** with synthetic soft, normal, loud, peak-heavy, silent/noisy, long-gap, short/invalid and repeated takes; confirms output ceiling, no repeat gain compounding, Wacky unchanged and native meter re-scale. CI retains N7c–N7m, R01–R08, Swift parse and production Web build. **Synthetic Linux tests cannot prove Apple SDK compilation, iPhone speaker/AirPods loudness, real children speaking, or parity with narration. R09b owner iPhone: NOT TESTED.** [TESTING.md](TESTING.md) checks a safe adult test phrase spoken softly and normally at fixed mic distance and device volume, relative to nearby narrated instructions on speaker and AirPods; no hiss/crackle/pumping/clipped consonants, unchanged Wacky effects, stable repeated Play, 16-bar responsiveness (not 100% demand), interruptions, app switching, route changes and R01–R08. **FLASH-05 is not closed until the owner reports comfortable actual iPhone voice playback.** Next distinct roadmap step remains **R10 `READ-04`** after acceptance; stable `ios-prep` and `main` are untouched. Production `com.andydoane.biblozoo` holds **10 private Zookeepers** and separate Audio Test has **11 different** profiles: keep independent private off-device backups, no uninstall/reset/restore/import, no voice/profile uploads or telemetry, and separately authorize any production-ID installation.

**R09b physical iPhone acceptance checklist (no private child voice upload, report PASS / FAIL / NOT TESTED):**

1. Only after owner PR review/merge and separately approved production-ID install with the correct original **10-Zookeeper** off-device private backup (not Audio Test's **11 different** profiles), use the usual GitHub Desktop Pull Origin, build, Capacitor sync and Xcode iPhone workflow. No uninstall/reset/import or uploaded profile/voice data.
2. At the same iPhone hardware/system volume and fixed mouth-to-mic distance, record a short **non-private adult test phrase** first in a **normal indoor voice**, then softly, then loudly. Listen to each native **Play My Recording**, ideally directly before/after the relevant **Flashcards narrated MP3 instructions** on the **speaker** and separately **AirPods**. Goal: a normal child's speaking level should be clearly audible without shouting; exact matching to a produced MP3 or a Web/Safari take is not claimed. Web comparison is optional at the same route/level.
3. Watch the meter while speaking. Ordinary speech should light useful bars, loud speech should usually reach upper bars. At the owner's previous approximate -27.5 dBFS RMS, the old 50% becomes ~64% (10/16); **100% is NOT required**. Silent room has low/off bars. Meter behavior must not trigger playback/session changes.
4. Listen to quiet, normal and loud dry takes twice. Boost should be clearly more helpful than merged #137; no sudden loud consonants, distortion, pumping, bright harshness or excessive room hiss in gaps. Repeating Play must not grow louder or add compression to saved PCM. `getCaptureState` optional numeric-only `rmsDbFS`, `peakDbFS` and `dryReplayGain` are *diagnostic* and the gain is a **maximum dry makeup setting**, not a calibrated playback loudness measurement.
5. Compare **Wacky Replay, Chorus and DEV lab** with previous owner baseline: original pitch/speed/effect loudness must remain unchanged. Re-recording, time-limit, empty/short recording, microphone permission failure, Stop/Exit and 3-2-1 countdown still work with no voice uploaded or written to profiles.
6. Recheck R01–R08 recovery: AirPods disconnect and fresh Play, Apple Music/external remote takeover, incoming phone call, held App Switcher/lock, Welcome Back exactly once on interruption, no automatic recording/replay, no background ghost audio, original Flashcards/Home pops, Read Tap in Order and Unscramble and Balloons/Fish. Do not infer optional scenario coverage if not tried.

**Decision:** If still barely audible, overly compressed or noisy, record the observation and keep `FLASH-05` open; don't push stronger gain or adjust `.measurement` automatically. R10 should wait until the owner accepts the voice audibility behavior.

## HISTORICAL R09 / FLASH-05 — measured, bounded native Flashcards dry replay level (PR #137; iPhone NOT TESTED)

**R08 owner physical-iPhone PASS / FIXED / CLOSED:** [PR #136](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/136) merged to `integration/native-audio-to-ios-prep` as `d4714f0d4aa7db9bc3af17d047bba7f6ff6e4ca9`. The owner reports both Tap in Order (`READ-05`) and Unscramble (`READ-06`) now **work great** on their physical iPhone. R01–R07 were already owner accepted. Exact locally installed SHA/iOS version and full optional matrix weren't supplied. The earlier R08 `NOT TESTED` text below predates this report.

**R09 / `FLASH-05` owner observation, not a calibrated measurement:** Flashcards' native dry voice recording sounded substantially quieter than the earlier Web recording. The browser path uses `getUserMedia({audio:true})` and MediaRecorder; native `BibloZooMicSessionProbePlugin` retains unprocessed mono Float PCM while `AVAudioSession` uses `.playAndRecord/.measurement`, and the child's dry `playCapture` copied the PCM unchanged to `AVAudioPlayerNode`. [Apple's measurement-mode documentation](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/avfaudio/avaudiosession/mode-swift.struct/measurement) notes that the mode reduces system signal/dynamics processing and can lower output levels. That is a plausible explanation, **not proof** the owner's recording is quieter because of that setting, its input route, hardware gain or output. Do not change capture session category, system volume, `.soloAmbient` output, microphone processing or route without separate evidence.

**[R09 PR #137](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/137), `fix/r09-flashcards-measured-dry-replay-level` → integration only:** At normal completed capture, on the native **capture worker only**, compute full-file RMS/true peak and a speech-level activity gate from the already-retained RAM samples. The optional dry-playback gain is applied **only if the take is objectively quiet**, has enough above-floor signal, no nonfinite samples and no high transient; gain is limited to **1.0–1.8×** (maximum +5.1 dB) *and* the original true peak times gain may not exceed **0.84 full scale**. During the next deliberately child-tapped **Play My Recording**, apply this scalar solely to a new, bounded, output-side PCM buffer, with a 0.84 defensive sample clamp. The in-RAM saved recording is never altered, replay never compounds, all Wacky/chorus/DEV effects use the original PCM, silence/noise/loud or clipped takes stay at gain 1. No JS voice bytes, files, uploads, retained child recordings, audio-session changes, auto-start, volume slider, capture-meter behavior, scoring or profile writes. The existing read-only native `getCaptureState` exposes only rounded integer `rmsDbFS`, `peakDbFS` and `dryReplayGain` for optional owner-level numeric diagnostics; these reset on discard/next take and are not shown to the child or logged.

**Verification and decision gate:** `scripts/validate_r09_flashcards_measured_dry_level.js` compiles/runs the **actual Swift level estimator and original dry PCM copy path** with synthetic sine speech/noise/silence/hot transient/invalid samples, repeated dry replay and unchanged Wacky PCM; wired into `.github/workflows/ios-prep-validation.yml` with R01–R08, N7c/D, Swift parse, safety/privacy and production Web build. A Linux Swift mock is **not** actual iPhone AGC, speaker/AirPods gain, Apple SDK compilation or objective native-versus-Web comparison. **R09 owner iPhone acceptance: NOT TESTED.** [TESTING.md](TESTING.md) requires matched phrase, mic distance, system volume and route on native Flashcards versus Web/Safari plus normal/loud/quiet/no speech, AirPods/speaker, repeated Play, effects, interruption, level/clipping and profiles. If gain is ineffective or amplifies noise, don't mark fixed or silently raise gain; report for an isolated refinement. **R10 `READ-04`** is the next separate roadmap slice once R09 is accepted. Original production `com.andydoane.biblozoo` has **10 private Zookeepers**, separate Audio Test app has **11**; keep independent private off-device backups, don't uninstall/import/reset or share child voice/private JSON; **separately authorize any production-ID phone install**. Stable `ios-prep` and `main` untouched.

**Owner real iPhone R09 acceptance — report PASS / FAIL / NOT TESTED, no private recording uploads:**

1. After reviewing/merging [PR #137](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/137), select `integration/native-audio-to-ios-prep` in GitHub Desktop, Pull Origin, and build/sync/open as before (`npm ci` as needed, `npm run build`, `npx cap sync ios`, `npx cap open ios`). **Do not install over the production-ID iPhone app without separate owner approval and an off-device backup** of its original 10 Zookeepers (not the separate 11-profile Audio Test data). No app uninstall/reset/import or child voice/profile data in chat/GitHub.
2. On the same iPhone, with system volume and environment fixed, make a short **adult/test phrase** at the same mouth-to-mic distance in native Flashcards and Safari/Web Flashcards. Use normal **Play My Recording** on each and compare perceived intelligibility and loudness on **speaker**, then **AirPods** at matched route volume. The native dry take should sound closer to Web, **not distorted, clipped, harsh or unexpectedly loud**. Do **not** use a child's actual voice for shared evidence.
3. Repeat a **soft-spoken** take, an **ordinary** take, and a **loud** take at the same distance. A genuinely low but valid dry take may get modest replay boost; already-loud speech, silence, background-only noise or sharp peaks should not become amplified. Numeric `getCaptureState` fields `rmsDbFS`, `peakDbFS`, `dryReplayGain` are optional read-only debugging values only; there is no child-facing level display and no PCM returned.
4. Play the same dry take **twice**: equal level each time, no gain compounding. Check **Wacky Replay** and chorus variants still sound like original DSP (never leveled), normal Record→3-2-1 countdown, recording meter, Stop/Replay, discard/re-record, long valid take up to original time limit and fail/retry state. The saved RAM samples are never modified.
5. Verify AirPods disconnect and return, Apple Music remote Play, App Switcher/lock/unlock, incoming call and `.soloAmbient` ownership: **no automatic recording or playback**, original one Welcome Back and retry behavior, no stolen audio or ghost callbacks. Smoke Read R08 cues, Balloons/Fish R07, Flashcards R01–R03, Web/PWA/Android recording and profiles/BibloPets. No early progress or skipped turns.

**Decision:** Note native-vs-Web comparison route, relative volume/quality, any distortion/noise, optional **numeric-only** gain/RMS/peak, approximate device version if known. R09 **remains NOT TESTED until physical owner comparison**; if it sounds too low or too loud, ask for a follow-up instead of increasing gain without measurements.

## HISTORICAL R08 / READ-05 + READ-06 — native Read first-note and melody clock recovery ([PR #136](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/136)) — owner iPhone NOT TESTED

**R07 owner iPhone PASS / FIXED / CLOSED:** [PR #135](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/135) was merged into `integration/native-audio-to-ios-prep` at `c040dcaa64132e876f554472abedc9470ddf069d`. The owner explicitly reports that **Balloons and Fish both work as intended after the merge and real iPhone test**. This closes `READ-02` and `READ-03`, following previously accepted R01–R06. The actual installed device SHA, iOS version and optional complete hardware matrix were not supplied; don't infer other Read cues passed. Earlier R07 “NOT TESTED” notes below are historical, not current status.

**Current R08 owner symptoms:** **Tap in Order** (`READ-05`) can lose notes on taps 1–4 of a five-word screen after AirPods/Apple Music/App Switcher interruption, then sound on tap five. **Unscramble** (`READ-06`) can collapse its two-note confirmation melody into almost simultaneous/rushed notes after return. iOS Read word feedback is **native AVAudioPlayer** output in `ios/App/App/SceneDelegate.swift`, not WebAudio. Apple's `AVAudioPlayer.play(atTime:)` requires a time *later* than the device clock; the clock can return to **zero** when idle. The old native code schedules the first note at `player.deviceCurrentTime + 0` (not a future time), uses a **different not-yet-playing player's clock** for each subsequent delay, and drops scheduled future voices with `!isPlaying` pruning. These are source-supported hazards; the exact on-device failure remains to be validated. Apple docs: [play(atTime:)](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/avfaudio/avaudioplayer/play(atTime:)) and [deviceCurrentTime](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/avfaudio/avaudioplayer/devicecurrenttime).

**Focused R08 [PR #136](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/136), `fix/r08-read-native-cue-timing-after-interruption` → integration only:** Pre-decode and `prepareToPlay()` all fixed authored cue notes before the first sound. Start the first note **immediately** via `play()` after the existing real child Start/Let's go audio-session claim; capture its live `deviceCurrentTime` once and schedule remaining notes at **that same clock + the original 0.09/0.1/0.18/0.19/0.27/0.3s offsets**. Keep the original MIDI notes, envelope, gain, duration, 12-voice cap and native only audio. Don't discard scheduled-but-not-yet-playing AVAudioPlayers: delegate completion and original interruption/Exit/stopCues cancel them, no stale backlog or auto reactivation. DEV-only waveform comparison uses the same safe timing helper. No AVAudioSession category, mic, recording, profile, score, notes volume, JS Web/PWA/Android fallback or session-policy changes. `READ-04` comparative loudness is separately planned R10.

**Automated gate:** Run `node scripts/validate_r08_read_native_cue_timing.js` (compiles actual Swift helper against a zero-device-clock AVAudioPlayer stub and VM-executes native JS lifecycle/cue dispatch); keep N7f/N7f2, N7m, R01–R07, all other regressions, Swift syntax and production Web build green. A simulated clock is not proof of speaker output, Mac Xcode SDK compilation or a successful real AirPods route.

**Owner real iPhone R08 acceptance — report `READ-05` and `READ-06` separately as PASS / FAIL / NOT TESTED:**

1. **Device and family safety:** Review/merge only [PR #136](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/136) into `integration/native-audio-to-ios-prep`; in GitHub Desktop Pull Origin, then `npm ci` if needed, `npm run build`, `npx cap sync ios`, `npx cap open ios`. Confirm original App scheme/bundle `com.andydoane.biblozoo`. Installing on original profile-bearing iPhone needs separate explicit owner authorization and private safe off-device **10-profile** original backup (distinct from the **11-profile** Audio Test). Never uninstall/reset/import/restore or expose child voices/profile details in repo/chat.
2. **Tap in Order / `READ-05`:** Use normal **Review → Learn → Listen Activities → Tap in Order** and a verse/chunk with **five distinct words**. After Apple Music starts by **AirPods Play**, wait for one Welcome Back, keep it for **10–15 seconds**, stop competing media and deliberately tap **Let's go!**. Tap words **one through five slowly**, one at a time. **Expected:** first word note audible immediately, every next correct tap has its own distinct timely note, no first-four silence/fifth burst, no delayed backlog, no doubled score. Finish chunk and check completion flourish's distinct rising note sequence. Try wrong taps independently.
3. **Unscramble / `READ-06`:** Repeat the identical audio interruption/Welcome Back sequence in Unscramble on a multiword chunk. Each correct unscramble emits **two distinct notes ~100ms apart**, not a simultaneous hit, rushed stutter, or extra delayed notes. Completion plays the original **four-note melody with 90/190/300ms offsets** relative to its first note; no arbitrary increase in loudness or score.
4. **Repeat and refusal:** Repeat both after **held App Switcher** with WebKit card still visible, lock/unlock, second AirPods interruption, and rapid valid taps. Audio may only restart after the existing real Let's go/native readiness gate, never on inactive/active callbacks. A failed/denied takeover must preserve Welcome Back/Retry and prevent early word credit or stale queued tones; after a fresh successful tap, old queued cues must not burst.
5. **Regression matrix:** Test muted mode, native speaker versus AirPods at matching system volume (note actual perceived loudness separately under future `READ-04/R10`), Read Balloons/Fish R07, Verse Crawl, Typewriter/Keyboard, existing read narration, Flashcards R01–R03, Daily R05, Verse Typer R06, and Web/PWA/Android note playback. No hidden WebAudio/HTML output on native iOS, no changes to `.soloAmbient`, saved profiles or BibloPet progress.

**Record:** Separate Tap in Order/Unscramble PASS/FAIL/NOT TESTED, how long Welcome Back was held, whether external Apple Music was still audible, first cue latency/number of notes, pair spacing, completion melody, source SHA/iOS version if known. Do not upload private child audio, Zookeeper JSON, names or IDs. **Do not mark R08 fixed until post-merge owner iPhone verification.**

## HISTORICAL R06 / PLAY-01 — Playground Verse Typer freeze while App Switcher held (owner iPhone NOT TESTED; 2026-11-17 Detroit)

**Prior PR closure:** [PR #133](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/133) **merged** into `integration/native-audio-to-ios-prep` at `f14ba11a733a9abd0566d6a4bb3e798d3c3d67af`. Owner has now confirmed **“Everything worked as you described. I think we can mark this one fixed.”** Record **R05 / `DAILY-01` and its bundled `LEARN-MENU-PAUSE` owner physical-iPhone PASS / FIXED**. R04 `LEARN-02` and R01–R03 are also owner-accepted. Earlier R05 “NOT TESTED” sections below are **historical** after this report; don't infer R06 or other unrelated issues passed or that an exact local device SHA/iOS version was supplied.

**R06 reproduction and source diagnosis:** Enter **Playground → Verse Typer** in the normal child-facing route, begin a typing round and reach the **Bible book-name caterpillar** with animated head/segments/tail. Enter and **hold** the iPhone App Switcher card view 10–15 seconds (do not select another app). Owner observed visible head/line/caterpillar colors continue changing in the thumbnail. Existing `native-verse-typer-audio.js` already notifies the existing `playgroundWelcome` of UIKit `active:false` even when WebKit stays visible; `pauseTyperForReturn()` already freezes `welcomeClock`, input and `trackedTimeout`. It does **not** stop `game.css` compositor keyframes for caterpillar tail yellow/orange/red/rainbow, crawl and wiggle, sparkle, butterfly or in-flight CSS transitions; some pending position/entrance `requestAnimationFrame` callbacks can run after inactive. **Only visual drift was physically observed; accidental scoring was not proven**, so don't claim a repaired scoring defect.

**Focused [R06 PR #134](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/134) implementation:** On the existing native/visibility `onPause` path in `verse_playground/verse_typer/game.js`, capture **running** animations from `#app.getAnimations({subtree:true})`, pause those original CSS/WAAPI players and add scoped `.vt-return-visual-paused` class on the root; `game.css` freezes all `#app` keyframes including pseudo-elements and newly created animated nodes, without affecting the separate Welcome Back overlay. Defer queued caterpillar entrance and skip stale reposition RAF while `returnPaused`/hidden. On real successful **Let's go!** and native audio readiness, resume the saved game clock and **the same animation players at their saved currentTime**; never auto-recreate the motion or grant credit. A failed native readiness/progress check keeps the existing overlay and all visuals frozen; Mode/Exit clears stale pause. Original timing, score, badge/reward paths, muted behavior, game-menu selection, Verse Jam/other Playground, Web/PWA/Android audio and AVAudioSession categories unchanged.

**Automated test:** `node scripts/validate_r06_playground_verse_typer_visual_freeze.js` wired into `.github/workflows/ios-prep-validation.yml`. It VM-executes the real native Verse Typer callback, pause/resume visual owner, existing `trackedTimeout` game clock and queued animation rAF; holds UIKit inactive 13.5 simulated seconds with `document.hidden=false` and checks original WAAPI currentTime, CSS class, unchanged book word index/correct-letters/typos/streak/completed, no callback fires, one recovery after child gesture/native `isReady`, exact remaining active deadline, repeat interruption, unchanged Web visibility recovery. Retain `scripts/validate_native_playground_verse_typer_n6n.js`, `validate_playground_welcome_back_patch67.js`/`patch68.js`, all R01–R05 and production web build. Linux VM/Swift grammar cannot verify compositor rendering on a real iOS phone or Xcode SDK typechecking; **R06 physical-iPhone acceptance is NOT TESTED until owner retest.**

Confirm original **App** scheme / intended bundle `com.andydoane.biblozoo`. **Separately authorize** any install over the original production-ID phone app; first retain the private off-device **10-Zookeeper** original app backup. The independent **11-profile Audio Test** export/app must never be used as the production restore. No uninstall, reset, import, photo/profile IDs, backup or private recorded voice in GitHub.

**Owner real-iPhone R06 acceptance — record PASS / FAIL / NOT TESTED for each:**

1. **Bible-book caterpillar:** From a normal Playground Verse Typer round, stop partway through typing the Bible book name while head/segments/tail are visibly animated. Open the iPhone App Switcher and **hold the BibloZoo card visible 10–15 seconds** without selecting another app. Look at the tail's pulsing stage and head/segments/ripple. **Expected: all movement and color/brightness changes freeze immediately**, not one blinking part advancing while the rest is still; letters typed and caterpillar position unchanged.
2. **Explicit resume:** Return from App Switcher. Expect **one existing Welcome Back** overlay. Do not tap it for several seconds: no hidden movement/score/audio progression. Tap **Let's go!** once. When real native sound is ready, the caterpillar resumes **at the same visual frame and Bible-book position**, with only remaining active gameplay time; no jump, skip, repeated entered letter, bonus or surprise badge. If native sound isn't ready, the existing Retry/Back must keep the frame frozen until a successful fresh gesture.
3. **Transitions and repeat:** Repeat while the caterpillar **crawls in/out**, tail is **yellow/orange/red/rainbow**, during a ripple or a typed-letter hop, and then hold a second App Switcher. Check the exact color/transition frame is still at the pre-interruption position, and that no delayed word advancement/typed credit occurs during 10+ seconds away. Verify the next word only begins after a real correct typing action and its remaining animation.
4. **Other Verse Typer paths:** Test Beginner and Advanced and complete a full verse. Check keyboard typing, hint head tap, wrong/right cues, native audio chunk review, mute toggle, **Game Menu → Close**, Help/Mode, the cocoon→butterfly sequence and normal completion reward. Normal menu pause is **not** the UIKit App Switcher and shouldn't invent Welcome Back. Confirm Safari/Web/PWA mode still works with the visibility Welcome Back and unchanged audio. Check normal game pause while actually backgrounding or locking.

No unearned Playground completion/score, no auto-start music or Bluetooth takeover.

## CURRENT R05 / DAILY-01 + R04 Learn-menu follow-up — iPhone acceptance PENDING (2026-11-17 Detroit)

**Owner acceptance recorded:** [R04 PR #132](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/132) **merged** into `integration/native-audio-to-ios-prep` at `4f8808c1a231ed8afb85ccb60063f90dc319d913`. The owner explicitly states **everything about PR #132 works on the real iPhone**, so the **held-App-Switcher Missing Words `LEARN-02` test is PASS / CLOSED**. The previous R04 pre-acceptance checklist below is now historical, not an outstanding gate. **New separate observation, deliberately bundled with R05:** during an already-running/pulsing **Say the Verse Out Loud** countdown, ordinary **Learn Menu → Open** should freeze it until **Menu → Close**. That in-app menu-pause behavior was not covered by the R04 App Switcher pass; its new code is **NOT YET iPhone tested**. Keep `LEARN-01` duplicate busy-phrase issue independently open.

**R05 [PR #133](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/133) root cause and candidate:** Original `DAILY-01` symptom: Daily Questions **Check the Verse → Listen**, held App Switcher interrupts voice but leaves bottom **Listening…** disabled, no Welcome Back; child must close/reopen verse panel. UIKit `willResignActive` already emits Swift `biblozooAppLifecycle(active:false)` even if WebKit remains `document.hidden=false`. Daily's Patch 71 `dailyQuestionReturn` never subscribed to that native event, so `onPause`/native preview cancellation/visible button redraw didn't run. Moreover `app.js playVerseDetailListen`'s native event guard skipped `Screen.DAILY_SESSION` entirely, so even a normally **completed** native reference→verse might never resolve the Daily `Listening…` state. A stale cancelled async `finally` could also clear the button on a later new Listen of the same verse. The R05 source fix feeds real native inactive/active into **the existing Daily one-button Welcome Back**, adds Daily to the existing native verse event allowlist, immediately cancels/repaints `verseAudioPlaying=false` on interruption, and invalidates old requests by **session-object + attempt token**. External media native `interrupted` while UIKit stays active also drives Welcome Back. Recovery does **not** silently replay, change the question/answer/reward or grant credit; genuine asset/bridge errors keep a plain manual Listen retry. Native-only listener; original Web/PWA/Android UI/media paths remain.

**Bundled Learn Menu follow-up:** `openLearnMenu()` now freezes the already-existing R04 HIDE `appReturnPausedAt` clock and CSS/WAAPI animations, including pulsing and 180ms/900ms fades, **without creating an OS Welcome Back**. `closeLearnMenu()` resumes at the exact remaining time/animation frame, not after the elapsed wall time. If a genuine held App Switcher occurs **with the menu open**, Menu Close cannot bypass the pending real Welcome Back; duplicate pause can't erase the originally paused animation handles. If the child acknowledges Welcome Back while menu is still open, the timer remains frozen until the ordinary Menu Close tap. Other Learn modes and Final Recall retain their existing policies; local menu pause also works in Web/PWA/Android.

**CI and source tests:** `node scripts/validate_r05_daily_check_verse_native_recovery.js` runs actual `daily_questions.js` controller + its Listen click with mocked native event while WebKit remains visible, tests native reference/verse `completed` and `interrupted` from the **real** `app.js` playback source, late stale Promise, original Daily question index/reward, one Welcome Back, no auto-play, true asset failure and browser isolation. `node scripts/validate_r04_learn_menu_pause_followup.js` executes the actual Learn Menu + existing HIDE clock/animation controller with timed close and overlapping UIKit interruption, including Web menu behavior. Both wired into `.github/workflows/ios-prep-validation.yml`; retain Patch 71, R01–R04, N7k and production Web CI. GitHub Swift source parsing and Node VM do **not** prove Xcode SDK compilation, native media audibility, actual held App Switcher or animation on a real phone. **R05 and menu-followup owner hardware acceptance: NOT TESTED.**

**Installation prerequisites (after owner PR review/merge, then a separate approval for production-ID iPhone update):** In GitHub Desktop select `integration/native-audio-to-ios-prep`, Pull Origin to its newly merged head, run `npm ci` if required, `npm run build`, `npx cap sync ios`, `npx cap open ios`. Verify the intended Xcode **App** scheme and original bundle `com.andydoane.biblozoo`. Keep a **private off-device backup of original 10-Zookeeper production data**, distinctly separated from the independent **11-profile Audio Test app**; do **not** uninstall/reset/reimport. Family-v2 export doesn't include separate Zoo surprise/rescue totals. Never post children's voices, profile identifiers, backups or recordings to GitHub/chat.

**Owner device acceptance — report PASS / FAIL / NOT TESTED per distinct case:**

1. **R05 primary (held App Switcher):** From the ordinary Daily Questions task, open a question's **Check the Verse** panel. Tap **Listen to the Verse**; confirm its reference→verse audio actually starts and the bottom says **Listening…**. While it is audibly speaking, open and **hold** the iPhone App Switcher for 10–15 seconds without leaving BibloZoo. Expected: voice stops; the visible/returned Check Verse panel resets to **Listen to the Verse**, not disabled **Listening…**; no question answer/progress changes.
2. **Return:** Come back to BibloZoo. Expect **one normal Welcome Back**, not a red audio asset failure, with the **same verse/question** and no automatic sound. Tap **Let's go!**; popup dismisses but voice remains stopped. A **fresh deliberate Listen tap** restarts reference then complete verse audibly, then button automatically returns to **Listen to the Verse** after true native completion. Do **not** need to close/reopen Check Verse.
3. **Native music interruption while still foreground:** With AirPods/Apple Music, repeat **Check the Verse → Listen** and press AirPods **Play** during voice. The real native interruption should cause the same Welcome Back with no ghost Listening state. Do not automatically re-activate or stop the competing music; the child may pause Apple Music and explicitly acknowledge/retry. Repeat interruption/retry twice and check that an old cancelled Listen result never resets a newer one.
4. **Normal/error/other Daily paths:** Uninterrupted native ref→verse Listen must **finish** (no permanent Listening); Close and reopen Check Verse should still work. A genuine unavailable/corrupt audio asset (only test if safely reproducible without editing family phone resources) must leave explicit Listen retry and **not** show false Welcome Back. Independently test a question **Play Sound** cue under held switcher and ordinary Daily answer/Next/reflect; question index, answer, earned-star/reward state and Feeding transition remain correct and are not credited by voice interruptions.
5. **R04 menu follow-up:** Go to Learn → **Missing Words**, remove a word, and let **Say the Verse Out Loud** pulse while its countdown is running. Tap the ordinary **Learn Menu** and leave it **open 10–15 seconds**. Underlying text, pulsing/opacity animation, countdown and hidden-word count must **freeze**. Tap **Close**: the *same* timer position and animation continue for their remaining active time, no word silently removed, **no Welcome Back** for this normal menu action. Repeat while **Remove Another** fades/transitions, and try opening/closing twice.
6. **Menu plus OS interruption:** While that Learn Menu is open over active Missing Words, hold App Switcher then return. A genuine UIKit interruption must still require **Welcome Back → Let's go!**, and **Menu Close alone** must not resume the underlying countdown before the separate OS recovery is acknowledged. If the OS prompt is acknowledged while the menu is still open, the countdown must stay frozen until Menu Close. No duplicate prompt, timer acceleration or spurious completion.
7. **Regression and data:** Verify R04 held App Switcher freeze on Missing Words still passes, Learn normal Listen/Chunks/Echo behavior and Final Recall stay intact. Check previously accepted R01 UI pops, R02 Flashcards Welcome Back, R03 speaker playback; normal Web/PWA and Android menu behavior. Confirm original **10 production Zookeepers**, verses, BibloPets, Daily progress and Feeding rewards unchanged.

**If something fails:** Record which **Daily voice context** (Check Verse vs question Play Sound), whether the app was in held App Switcher or external Apple Music took over while foreground, whether label stuck, one/none/multiple Welcome Back prompts, whether a real Listen retry worked, and for Learn the exact menu/OS order and remaining timer. Include locally installed SHA/iOS version if known, **no private profile/voice data**. The owner alone can mark R05 `DAILY-01` and the small menu follow-up PASS; **R06 / `PLAY-01`** is next on the roadmap after that.

## CURRENT R04 / LEARN-02 — Learn Missing Words held App Switcher freeze (2026-11-17 Detroit; iPhone NOT TESTED)

**Prerequisite closure:** Owner merged [R03 PR #131](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/131) to `integration/native-audio-to-ios-prep` (`dcd09646be4e2e1017a2906d82254b6a5b627d50`) and explicitly reports **all R03 physical-iPhone tests PASSED**, so `FLASH-04` is **FIXED**. R01 and R02 were previously owner-accepted. This does not prove R04 or the later Daily/Playground/Read items. Earlier R03 "NOT TESTED" checklists below are historical.

**R04 reproduction and source diagnosis:** On a learned verse's **Review → Learn → Missing Words** activity, enter the timed “Say the Verse Out Loud” phase / repeated **Remove a Word / Remove Another** progress, then swipe into and **hold** the iPhone App Switcher showing BibloZoo's thumbnail **without selecting another app**. UIKit has `willResignActive` and Swift emits `biblozooAppLifecycle(active:false)`, but WebKit may still report `document.hidden=false`. Existing Patch 71 `appActivityReturn` subscribes only to visibility/page events, so `waitForActiveLearnTime()` and `getSayVersePct()` never see the paused Welcome Back state. CSS animated prompt/word removal and its 180ms/900ms wall-clock transitions can also continue. This is **LEARN-02**; a separate Learn menu Close is not an OS interruption.

**Focused R04 implementation ([PR #132](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/132)):** Native iOS only, `app.js` binds the existing shared app-activity Welcome Back to `biblozooAppLifecycle` **only while Missing Words is current**. UIKit inactive calls `pauseFromNativeInactive()` before WebKit becomes hidden; UIKit active calls `showAfterNativeActive()` (not `onContinue()`). The original overlay is one `Let's go!` button and Back to Title escape; no duplicate, no new audio. Freeze original `appReturnPausedAt` say-verse clock, running CSS keyframes/WAAPI transitions (in `styles.css`), and transition/metadata timers until explicit acknowledgement. While backgrounded or paused, even the last timer callback cannot advance the phase; the original “Remove Another” button and hidden-word plan resume from the same progress after the child taps Let's go. No recording/audio category, media activation, scoring, profiles, other Learn types, Web/PWA or Android output changes.

**Automated gate:** `node scripts/validate_r04_learn_missing_words_inactive_pause.js` added to `.github/workflows/ios-prep-validation.yml`. It VM-executes the actual Patch 71 `app.js` controller with native `active:false/true` while `document.hidden=false`, verifies timer fraction/word count/animation freeze under a 10+ tick hold, one pending Welcome Back with no automatic resume, exact clock timestamp restoration after tap, transitions guarded by active-time waits, current-screen/idle/FinalRecall/Web isolation. Keep existing `validate_patch71_daily_app_welcome_back.js`, `validate_native_learn_quiet_interruption_n7k.js`, R01–R03, Learn narration and full Node + Web production build green. Linux Swift parsing is not a Mac Xcode SDK compile, and VM tests do not prove held iPhone animation behavior.

**Owner Mac / phone preparation after review and merge only:** Use the **integration** GitHub Desktop checkout; Pull Origin, `npm ci` if needed, `npm run build`, `npx cap sync ios`, `npx cap open ios`. Verify the Xcode App scheme and bundle identity `com.andydoane.biblozoo`. **Separately authorize** updating the original production-ID phone installation and retain your own off-device private **10-Zookeeper** original production-family backup; the **11-profile** Audio Test backup belongs exclusively to the independent app. Do not uninstall, reset, import, expose voices or backups on GitHub, or assume family-v2 exports include separate Zoo surprise/rescue stats.

**Physical iPhone acceptance — record PASS / FAIL / NOT TESTED for each:**

1. With an existing Zookeeper and learned verse, open **Learn → Missing Words** through the normal child-facing route. Begin the word removal and **Say the Verse Out Loud** timed phase; while words/prompt are visibly animating, open and **hold the App Switcher** (do not release into another app) for **10–15 seconds**. Expected: BibloZoo's visible thumbnail **freezes immediately**, with the same words removed, no prompt pulsing, fading or extra phase advance while held. The UI should NOT silently finish the timer.
2. Return to BibloZoo. Expected: **one standard Welcome Back** over the original Missing Words screen; **no automatic resume or new word removal**. Pause any competing Apple Music if it is playing, then explicitly tap **Let's go!**. The activity should continue from **the same fractional timer position/visible word plan and animation frame**, not skip to the next word, consume the 10–15 paused seconds or issue a grade/reward. Check the prompt resumes normally and can finish once after the remaining active time.
3. Repeat with a **second held App Switcher** during a new timed attempt. Check a single overlay and preserved state again. Also switch during the **brief Remove Another fade-out/fade-in** or initial phase before its timer starts; the fade/word count should wait for explicit acknowledgement rather than complete invisibly.
4. **Menu regression:** In Missing Words, open the normal in-app Learn menu and tap **Close** without using App Switcher. Expected: ordinary menu Close works, **no Welcome Back popup**. Try completing Missing Words normally without any switch; removed words, text layout, button labels and final transition to **Final Recall** stay unchanged. A final real child action must be required; interruption must not award an extra completion.
5. **Isolation regression:** Check Learn **Listen**, **Chunks**, and **Echo** recover as previously accepted; briefly verify Final Recall timer and normal full-background/lock return prompt. R01 Flashcards UI pops, R02 instructions/verse Welcome Back and R03 AirPods speaker replay should remain intact. Web/PWA and Android should not gain an iOS-specific interruption listener or new popup.
6. **Data safety:** All original **10 production Zookeepers**, selected verses, BibloPets, Daily rewards and saved progress remain intact. No raw child voice, backup data, usernames, recording contents or personal identifiers are required for a bug report.

**Report if FAIL:** Exact phase (auto-start, fade, active timed prompt, or after finish), whether WebKit's thumbnail remained visible, how long the switcher was held, if text/animation moved, which prompt appeared (or did not), whether a new word was removed or time skipped after **Let's go!**, installed integration SHA and route/iOS version if available. ## CURRENT R03 / FLASH-04 — Native recorded voice replay after AirPods disconnect (2026-11-18; owner iPhone NOT TESTED)

**Prerequisite status:** Owner merged [R02 PR #129](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/129) and [follow-up #130](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/130) (`integration/native-audio-to-ios-prep` at `8e14c18b90cf8c4134f826d8e26479b507329e7b`) and expressly reports **both `FLASH-02` and `FLASH-03` FIXED on physical iPhone**. Earlier R02 "partial/pending" sections below describe historical observations only. This is not proof R03, loudness `FLASH-05` or Silent Mode work.

**Review [PR #131](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/131). Exact R03 issue:** The owner previously recorded a verse using AirPods, disconnected AirPods **after** completing the take but **before** pressing **Play My Recording**, and heard **silence** from the expected iPhone speaker. Captured PCM should be safely retained in RAM, not converted to files or uploaded. R03 does not re-record, change voice volume or automatically force the speaker. It changes the Swift playback-start route handshake to build the engine only after the child's user-tap `AVAudioSession.setActive(true)`, validates a usable current output, fences pending PCM copy and graph startup against genuine route changes, and treats `AVAudioEngineConfigurationChange` as an interrupted playback. The JavaScript recorder keeps the same recording and offers **“Audio output changed. Tap Play to try again.”** on route refusal. No fake `playbackComplete`, grading or Wacky eligibility; only a matching native consumed-buffer `ended` completes an actually playing voice. This is a plausible source-rooted fix candidate, **not** independently verified audible-device causality.

**Automated checks:** `node scripts/validate_r03_flashcards_route_replay.js` (in `.github/workflows/ios-prep-validation.yml`) audits the current Swift playback method/route observer and runs the **actual** native JS Flashcards recorder with mocked Play replies: AirPods-route-changed refusal, route-not-ready refusal, deliberate retry, in-flight output interruption, stale native end rejection, RAM-take survival, actual-end-only credit, original Web recorder and no browser output fallback. Keep R01, R02, N7c/N7d/N7d.2, Wacky N7e, Flashcards instructions and full production Web build green. GitHub Linux Swift parsing checks only **syntax**, not iOS SDK typecheck/Xcode linking or physical output routes; exact iPhone hearing needs owner test.

**Owner iPhone acceptance (after reviewing/merging focused integration PR and separately authorizing original-ID app update):**

1. Preserve a PRIVATE off-device original **10-Zookeeper** production family-v2 export. The independent Audio Test identity has **11** profiles—never import or conflate it. Family-v2 export excludes separate Zoo rescue/surprise counters. Do **not** uninstall, reset, import, or upload any family backup/voice sample. In GitHub Desktop pull `integration/native-audio-to-ios-prep` after merge; run `npm ci` if required, `npm run build`, `npx cap sync ios`, and `npx cap open ios`. Confirm the correct Xcode **App** scheme/bundle `com.andydoane.biblozoo`; updating that production-ID installation requires separate owner approval.
2. **Primary R03 repro:** Connect AirPods, go to **Practice → Flashcards**, choose a learned verse, press Record and finish a short real voice take. While **Play My Recording** is visible, disconnect the AirPods fully (Bluetooth or case), wait until the iPhone's **audio output selector** shows iPhone speaker, then tap **Play My Recording** **once**. Expected: the same take plays **audibly on iPhone speaker**, not to dead AirPods, with normal Play/Stop and Next gating. There must be no automatic replay/recording start while reconnecting or switching routes.
3. **Fast route-change race:** Repeat with AirPods disconnect immediately before tapping Play, while output route might still be switching. Either the voice becomes audible on the settled speaker, **or** the UI shows **“Audio output changed. Tap Play to try again.”** while retaining the take. On a **new deliberate tap** after the route settles, the same take should play audibly. A falsely “playing/completed” silent recording is a FAIL even if the button briefly animates. Do not count a failure to finish the interrupted playback as successful listening.
4. **During-playback loss:** With a fresh recording, start **Play My Recording** while AirPods are still connected, then remove/disconnect them during playback; repeat once within ~0.5s of Play and once after several seconds. Expected: the sound stops safely and shows a recoverable interruption rather than silently continuing/crediting an unheard take. After route settles, tap Play again and hear the same take on available output; the RAM take should survive. Check Bluetooth reconnect/replay afterward.
5. **Other paths:** With AirPods connected and with speaker alone, record and play normally to real end, including a short take and one official Wacky preset. Test an explicit Stop Playback and re-Play, a pause/background/held App Switcher, and an external Apple Music AirPods Play interruption: BibloZoo must not automatically reclaim other-app audio or play in background; Welcome Back/Retry requires deliberate gestures. Real native `ended` alone unlocks normal Next/grade; failed/rejected/route-changed replay must not unlock Wacky, fabricate Next, double-play or double-reward.
6. **Safety/smoke:** Confirm R01 Flashcards/Home UI pops after record, R02 instructions and **Listen to the Verse** Welcome Back, the **Replay Instructions** label/Next, normal Home and Daily activities, and the expected **10 production Zookeepers/progress/BibloPets** intact. No recordings/private files committed or transferred.

**Report:** Installed integration SHA, iOS version, where the output selector points after AirPods disconnect, speaker/headphone volume, audible PASS/FAIL/NOT TESTED for immediate and settled output, whether the UI showed Play/Stop/route error, and whether a subsequent explicit Play succeeded. No child's voice/audio recording, backup or profile identifier needed. R03 remains **NOT TESTED** until owner physical acceptance. R04 Learn held-switcher remains the independent next issue afterward.

**Context:** [PR #129](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/129) was merged to `integration/native-audio-to-ios-prep` (`58d01838351417bace0b03817922f3278a67b4ee`). In GitHub Desktop use **integration**, Pull Origin after merge, run `npm run build`, `npx cap sync ios`, open Xcode with `npx cap open ios`, and verify the intended app/bundle identity before updating your phone.
2. **Listen button / FLASH-03:** With AirPods and Apple Music, open Flashcards on a learned verse, tap **Listen to the Verse**, and press the AirPods Play control while the verse/reference audio is playing. Expected: **Welcome Back** without a red failure toast; after **Let's go!**, the button correctly reads **Listen to the Verse**, not **Stop Listening** (including accessible `aria-pressed=false`). Tapping it **once** should actually start the reference/verse audio, then show **Stop Listening** only while actively playing. Test both reference and full-verse interruption if possible.
3. **Instruction Next / FLASH-02:** In Flashcards pick No Help/First Letters/Pictures to reach its spoken instruction slide. While instructions are audibly playing, **Next stays disabled**, as before. Use AirPods Play to interrupt with Apple Music; Welcome Back should appear. Pause Apple Music, tap **Let's go!**, and verify **Next is now enabled / not faded** and **Replay Instructions** is available but optional. Tap **Next** without replay: the normal recording-intro screen opens, with the selected verse/difficulty intact. Repeat by choosing **Replay Instructions** instead and verify narration starts only from this explicit tap; Next stays disabled while that narration is playing and becomes available on true end.
4. **Negative checks:** Genuine audio errors (if reproducible without altering production assets) must still leave Next disabled and offer **Replay Instructions**; normal uninterrupted instructions must not skip early; ordinary navigation/Stop must not leave **Stop Listening** stuck, create false Welcome Back, duplicate sounds or score/reward. Repeat interruption and retry twice. Ensure Apple Music is not automatically taken over, R01 UI pops survive a recording, and all 10 profiles, progress and pets survive.
5. Record **PASS / FAIL / NOT TESTED** separately for **label reset, Next enablement, replay copy, genuine error gate, duplicate interruption and data preservation**; include installed build SHA and relevant route/iOS version if available, but **no children's voices, backups, Zookeeper names/IDs or private data**. This PR fixes UI-state recovery only; R03 route-changed recorded-take sound, R09 gain, and R15 Silent Mode remain separate.

## CURRENT R02 PR — Flashcards native Welcome Back for Apple Music/AirPods interruption (2026-11-17; OWNER IPHONE NOT TESTED)

**Issues:** `FLASH-02` (instruction narration) and `FLASH-03` (Flashcards **Listen to the Verse**). **Review [PR #129](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/129). Branch:** `fix/r02-flashcards-external-audio-welcome-back` → `integration/native-audio-to-ios-prep` only. Owner merged PR #128 and now personally reports `FLASH-01` **fixed on physical iPhone**, with [integration post-merge CI green](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/actions/runs/44194453573); that does **not** prove R02. The R02 changes have **not been hardware tested**.

**Source diagnosis:** While UIKit remains active, external Apple Music/AirPods Play can cause Swift to invalidate narration and emit a real `biblozooAppLifecycle` `reason:"audio-interruption"`. Flashcards instructions previously mapped this to generic `"failed"` and its red “Instructions couldn't play” toast. The native ref/whole-verse Listen controller already emitted `"interrupted"` but `app.js` reduced it to boolean `false`; Flashcards' async Listen button displayed another generic audio-failure toast. R02 feeds both genuine interruption events into the existing Flashcards Welcome Back, revokes the pending Listen request, and uses the Swift event as a backup for foreground-only interruptions. Real missing file/decoder/bridge failures still show normal retry, and Web/PWA/Android remain on their original audio path. No AVAudioSession category change or automatic audio reclaim/replay.

**Automated gate:** `node scripts/validate_r02_flashcards_welcome_back.js`, added to `.github/workflows/ios-prep-validation.yml`. It tests instruction `reason:"interrupted"` vs a real native decode failure, existing retry/success gating, native verse `onInterrupted`, one Welcome Back despite duplicate native signals, pending Listen cancellation and no erroneous red toast, guarded Swift `.began` lifecycle fallback, no false prompt on unrelated route notifications, unchanged Web behavior and no Flashcards overlay in ordinary Verse Detail. Existing N5b/N7d/N7f/R01 tests and production Web build remain required. GitHub Node/Web PASS is **not** evidence of real iPhone speaker output, iOS Swift compile or AirPods behavior.

**Mac / iPhone preparation (only after the owner reviews and merges this PR):** Do not modify `ios-prep` or `main`. In the **integration** GitHub Desktop worktree, Fetch/Pull this branch's merged integration head, run `npm ci` if needed, then `npm run build`, `npx cap sync ios` and `npx cap open ios`. Confirm Xcode App target uses the intended `com.andydoane.biblozoo` identity, and build/Archive as appropriate. **Before updating the original production-ID iPhone app, obtain the owner's separate explicit approval** and retain an off-device private production-family export. Original production has **10 Zookeepers**; the independent Audio Test app has **11** and its backup must not be imported into production. Family-v2 exports omit standalone Zoo surprise/rescue totals. Never uninstall, reset or overwrite saved data to investigate audio. Keep children's voice/backup files out of GitHub and logs.

**Owner device acceptance checklist — record exact installed SHA, iOS version, output route and PASS/FAIL/NOT TESTED separately:**

1. With the iPhone Silent switch OFF, AirPods connected and volume audible, open an existing production Zookeeper and go to **Practice → Flashcards**. Pick a learned verse. Begin an audible **difficulty/recording instruction** (e.g. No Help/First Letters/Pictures explanation). While the **instructions are actually speaking**, use the **AirPods remote Play** command to make Apple Music resume. Do **not** task-switch out of BibloZoo. Expected: the same standard **Welcome Back** overlays the preserved Flashcards view; no transient or persistent red **“Instructions couldn't play”** toast; narration stops and Apple Music is not forcibly paused or reclaimed by the app. **This is FLASH-02.**
2. Pause Apple Music yourself, return to the active BibloZoo view if needed, tap **Let's go!** once. Expected: overlay dismisses without automatically restarting instructions; the ordinary **Try Instructions Again** control explicitly restarts valid narration. Confirm the screen still has the same selected verse, difficulty and current task; Next/credit was not advanced by a rejected or interrupted sound.
3. Independently start from Flashcards' **How do you want to say the verse? → Listen to the Verse** button. During **reference** playback, press AirPods remote Play to resume Apple Music; confirm **Welcome Back** rather than **“Audio couldn't play”**. Pause Apple Music, tap **Let's go!**, then deliberately tap **Listen** to hear it again from the start. If possible repeat during the **full verse** segment. **This is FLASH-03**, not automatically passed by FLASH-02.
4. Repeat an external Play interruption at least twice, including the normal "UIKit still active" case and a switch away/back. Verify only one Welcome Back, no ghost audio, no red late toast after acknowledgement, no frozen/disabled Listen button, no automatic Apple Music takeover, no counterfeit native completion, and **Back to Title** still safely exits. A normal child-initiated Stop/Back or in-app menu transition should not unexpectedly create an interruption popup.
5. Sanity regression: an ordinary successful full narration completes normally, real retry is available for an actual playback problem (do not delete/rename app audio resources on the family phone), a new short Flashcards voice take still plays, UI pops work after recording (R01), and the expected **10 production Zookeepers**, progress and BibloPets remain intact. No new Daily task, Feeding score or Zoo reward is credited by interruption/recovery.

**If failure:** Report whether it happened in **instructions** or **reference/full-verse Listen**, the precise sequence (AirPods Play while sound already speaking vs before start), whether the app thumbnail was still active, visible overlay/error, route/Apple Music state and whether an explicit retry worked. No child's voice, family backup, profile identifiers or private data are needed. **Only the owner can mark FLASH-02/03 FIXED after physical device retest.** R03 Bluetooth recorded-take playback and R15 Silent Mode are separate issues.

## HISTORICAL R01 PR — Flashcards UI pop restoration (owner-reported FIXED after PR #128, 2026-11-17)

**Issue ID:** `FLASH-01`. **Merged PR:** [#128](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/128), integration merge `0246d775e7`; owner now reports real iPhone retest PASS and considers the post-recording UI pops fixed. **The following paragraph/test plan is the historical pre-merge acceptance checklist.** No raw profiles or voice data were committed. The identified bug is a stale JS UI-pop `ready` cache after the recording microphone barrier deallocates the shared native PCM bank. A one-call invalidation now happens **before** that barrier. Only a future **real user tap** may activate/reload the UI samples; there is no autonomous audio session takeover or WebAudio fallback.

**CI status:** [PR #128 run 44191269823](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/actions/runs/44191269823) reports **Node regression PASS / production web build PASS** for the initial code/test revision. A newer documentation-only commit requires checking the latest run again. **Real iPhone audio NOT TESTED.**

**CI contract:** `node scripts/validate_r01_flashcards_ui_pop_handoff.js` uses the *actual* `native-ui-audio.js`, `flashcards/recording.js`, and `native-flashcards-capture-n7c.js` in a fake iOS Capacitor environment where `devPrepareForMicrophone` discards the PCM effect bank. It verifies the initial UI pop, real Record/Stop and retained RAM take, **no automatic activation/PCM rewarm after recording**, and **both pops re-preloaded on the next genuine eligible UI tap**, with subsequent taps audible; mic-permission denial must not drain the bank. Existing CI still covers native session handoff, Read, Feeding, Web/PWA and Swift syntax. These checks cannot prove real speaker audibility, iPhone session correctness or runtime microphone privacy.

**Owner physical iPhone test (only after owner approves an updated production-ID installation):**
1. Confirm the expected **10 production Zookeepers** and keep an off-device private production-family backup before any update; never import the separate 11-profile Audio Test backup. On the revised integration build, have the **Silent switch OFF**, speaker volume audible, and close Apple Music/PiP for the normal baseline.
2. Open an existing profile; tap ordinary Home/menu buttons that usually make the two UI **pop** sounds. Open **Flashcards**, tap navigation buttons that normally pop *before* recording. Do not judge the deliberately silent **Record / Stop / Play My Recording** controls, which carry `data-no-ui-sound`.
3. Tap Record, allow the normal **3–2–1** countdown, speak a *nonprivate* short test phrase into the mic, Stop. Confirm the recording UI is sane; audio captured/replayed normally. **Do not send or save** the recorded voice.
4. Tap the ordinary *eligible* Flashcards navigation/Next/Back controls after capture: UI pops should recover instead of remaining permanently silent. The first tap may perform a bounded asynchronous native warmup; a delayed/absent first pop is worth reporting, but **subsequent taps must consistently sound**. No double pops or stale sound from a prior screen.
5. Return to **main Home** and navigate several menus; UI pops must still sound. Reenter Flashcards, record/stop a **second** new take, and repeat Steps 4–5. No accumulation of silent state, no second mic permission prompt, no missing recordings or skipped progress.
6. **Safety smoke:** With AirPods connected and/or Apple Music playing, a true Record tap may claim audio, but a normal Stop must not **automatically resume or steal** external music. With locked/held App Switcher or genuinely interrupted capture, no phantom pop, recording restart or auto-output activation may occur. Existing Flashcards interruption-error and AirPods route bugs (`FLASH-02..04`) are **separate future roadmap items** and need not be falsely marked fixed by R01.
7. Verify 10 profiles/sampled verse progress and BibloPets unchanged; confirm optional Web/PWA/Android UI pops still work if available. Report **PASS / FAIL / NOT TESTED** with iPhone audio route, where pops stopped/returned, and whether the issue persisted after leaving Flashcards. Do not send backups, identifying data or voice clips.

**Do not mark R01 complete** from code/CI alone. The owner must merge the PR and explicitly authorize/install the revised production bundle ID before the on-device result can be recorded. Keep R02–R17 separate.

Build with `npm run build`, then re-run the new validator to check the generated guard. Swift `-parse` on Linux and GitHub Node/Web PASS are not Apple-SDK or physical iPhone verification. On production iOS, hidden DEV panels, forced pet flags (even stale storage), simulated score/recovery menus, secret progress-changing shortcuts and DEV voice variants must remain unavailable; the separate Audio Test preserves them. **Step 3 remains unfinished** pending Apple privacy manifest, permission/retention checks, resource membership, reachable diagnostics, accessibility and profile backup review. Do not install the production-ID candidate over an original app with valuable profiles.

**Updated 2026-11-15 integration evidence:** [Step 3b PR #114](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/114) was merged at `a9c6bd36`; [post-merge Actions #43774319618](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/actions/runs/43774319618) passed Node regression checks and Production Web build. `ios-prep` remains untouched. Step 3c [PR #115](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/115) (review-only, not merged) closes residual developer diagnostics: when the production native-output guard has `enabled=true, devToolsAllowed=false`, even a pre-existing `biblozooAudioDiagnostics=1` flag or URL `audioDiag=on` cannot initialize the legacy diagnostics monkeypatches/listeners/Audio log overlay. The native `BibloZooAudioSessionDiagnostics` plugin is only registered on Audio Test; direct production JS debug state/timing clipboard/mark-late/Jam trace attempts are rejected at Swift. The actual child-facing sound output, `getCuePlaybackState`, legacy recovery's native-disabled mutating path, and **real Flashcards mic handoff via `devPrepareForMicrophone`** remain unchanged. The unusual `devPrepareForMicrophone` name does **not** mean it is safe to remove: Flashcards production depends on it. Run `node scripts/validate_native_production_diagnostics.js`, `node scripts/validate_audio_diagnostics_patch55.js`, `node scripts/validate_native_sound_smoke_test.js`, the rest of CI and Linux Swift parse; record exact final PR head. **Not tested:** Mac Apple-SDK build, signed resources/Privacy Report, native recording/route/lock interruptions, data-preservation backup. **Baseline owner evidence:** [PR #105](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/105) is now **MERGED** as `fe02a513`; the owner independently retested **all previously failing Verse Typer active speech and Scripture Scrub mower active-sound lock scenarios with DEV Force Pet Escape ON and OFF on a real iPhone** and says all are working fine. No new source, Swift, ID, engine, `.soloAmbient` category change, AudioContext or HTML fallback on native. **Normal native Learn Listen and all four native Feeding modes still use their original plain Welcome Back** unless the existing **DEV Force Pet Escape** switch is ON. Learn Chunks/Echo keep their current eligibility. Never force Zoo in unrelated Learn screens or Daily-To-Do controls.

Original lesson narrator/quiet countdown and Feeding physics/tones, Melody note timing, Labyrinth tilt, Bounce collision window, score, daily snacks and reward checkpoints **remain frozen** while pets escape. Only **LOCK GATE** invokes the original activity owner within the separate real gesture: Learn must prove its actual source narration advances (or same quiet phase clock truly moves), Feeding must pass its existing mode-specific native preflight or advancing Web Audio clock before shifting deadlines and thawing. A cue-only play ACK or native session claim must NEVER credit score or dismiss the real game gate. If no surprise selected, same original single-gesture recovery. A failed provisional odds commit must not unpause the game. No duplicate Daily snack award or shifted Melody/physics deadlines on repeated lock.

**Automated tests:** New `node scripts/validate_learn_feeding_zoo_cue_transition.js` runs the production shared cue helper with native `pilot-unlock` two-position clock, no native HTML fallback, Web `Audio.currentTime`, mute, denied activation, stalled clocks, and stale second-lock cancellation. It also VM-executes the actual Learn recover function separately for Listen/Chunks/Echo to prove cue-only first gesture, preserved checkpoints, original owner only at Gate, no-surprise fallback; executes the real Feeding Zoo callback block for each Classic/Labyrinth/Bounce/Melody including owner refusal, no-surprise fallback, failed provisional commit, second lock, and original owner-only thaw. Run unchanged mode regressions `node scripts/validate_patch72_1b_feeding_zoo_escape.js`, `node scripts/validate_native_feeding_resume_n21.js`, `node scripts/validate_native_melody_gameplay_n4a.js`, `node scripts/validate_native_read_external_audio_n7m.js`, the full Learn/Read/Daily activity suites, native first-launch/Apple-Music competition scenarios, and standard GitHub Actions Web build plus Linux Swift grammar. Report **final PR head** CI separately from real Xcode/iPhone validation.

**Focused actual Audio Test iPhone checklist (one grouped PR, not seven separate builds):**
1. **Learn Listen:** DEV Force ON, begin active published verse narration, lock mid-voice, return, Let's go! hears *only* BibloPet unlock and NO early source words; finish escape and LOCK GATE, original verse resumes from same source/position. DEV Force OFF must remain the original plain Welcome Back without a pet.
2. **Learn Chunks:** Lock during native spoken part and separately during quiet between-clips gap. With an eligible Zoo surprise, cue only on first tap; same part/position or truly advancing quiet gap returns at LOCK GATE. No word/clip index skip, no duplicate playback, no hidden progress during pets. Repeat with DEV Force OFF to check normal eligibility/odds.
3. **Learn Echo:** Lock during native spoken part and during the yellow/silent “your turn to say it” countdown. Cue-only first tap; final Gate recovers the *same* exact native part or remaining quiet-turn interval; no false narration proof, no extra earned progress.
4. **Feeding Classic + Labyrinth:** With DEV Force ON lock mid-peg or fall/tilt when sounds are playing. Confirm entire board/score and collision timers stay frozen throughout cue and escape; after LOCK GATE original native bank is ready before movement resumes. No moved catchers, phantom points or recentered tilt.

Ensure no bonus-period timeout or Melody note phase advances behind the pet; only verified original sound recovery at Gate shifts true deadlines, not earned snack count. Lock again during cue, rescue, and final gate; with Apple Music/YouTube PiP/AirPods competing and an OS-denied session, game must stay frozen and accept a new actual tap or clean Back. Test one Web/PWA selected surprise and no-surprise return, including offline cached BibloPet sound; Web clocks must advance, not just accept play() promises.
