## Merged and real-iPhone verified [PR #105](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/105) — native UIKit inactive-first Zoo return

**Owner evidence (2026-11-14 Detroit / 2026-11-15 UTC):** [PR #104](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/104) **MERGED** at `9d81c34a`. The owner tested DEV Force Pet Escape on a real Audio Test iPhone. Most recommended games worked, but when the phone was locked **while sound was actively playing**, (1) **Verse Typer** could show a foreign gray `Native speech isn't ready. Tap Try Again.` dialog; Try Again never recovered and Back was required; (2) **Scripture Scrub** locked mid-mower sound could finish the Pet Escape and LOCK GATE yet refuse further grass taps, although the menu still worked. DEV Force OFF ordinary Welcome Back did **not** reproduce Typer's bad dialog. This observation is real-device evidence of a regression, not proof of the source-level cause.

**Root-level fix:** The PR #104 Zoo-specific adapter accidentally omitted `pauseFromNativeInactive` and `showAfterNativeActive`, which the old plain Welcome Back implements. Both Playground owners call these from their `BibloZooNative*Audio` UIKit `.inactive`/`.active` notifications, often **before** WebKit sets `document.hidden`. Add the same explicit pause/reveal-only API to the shared Zoo adapter. No audio is activated by resume-visible; the child must tap **Let's go** and final **LOCK GATE**, with original owner verification. Verse Typer's review-chunk loop reroutes lost readiness to shared Welcome Back when available rather than displaying a stranded native speech dialog. Scripture Scrub also retires a lost/invisible mower RAF and releases `mowerActive` only if no real resume callback exists—no grass, score, round progress or extra completion awarded.

**Automated coverage:** `node scripts/validate_shared_zoo_audio_cue_transition.js` now exercises an authentic UIKit inactive-first event **while `document.hidden` is still false**, duplicate inactive/visibility notifications, no duplicate pause, native selected surprise/cue, pending game owned Gate, and extractively executes the actual Typer and Scrub pause/resume functions: a native stopped narrator must hold its checkpoint until original PCM is ready; an active mower must freeze, restart its saved RAF/sound on Gate, or retire an orphan frame to reopen input without any synthetic grass. Run `node scripts/validate_native_playground_verse_typer_n6n.js` and `node scripts/validate_native_playground_scripture_scrub_n6o.js` plus the complete GitHub Actions Node, Linux Swift syntax and production Web build. Physical Xcode/native speaker/device behavior is **still owner PENDING**.

**Targeted actual iPhone retest:**
1. **Verse Typer**, DEV Force Pet Escape ON: enter a verse with **active spoken chunk narration**, lock the phone while the voice is audible, unlock; **Welcome Back** must be the only recovery dialog, not the strange gray native speech Retry. Tap Let's go, hear the original BibloPet unlock cue, finish the escape and **LOCK GATE**; only now must the original verse chunk and typing recover. No skipped review words, duplicate narration, gray dialog, repeating Try Again or silent progression.
2. **Scripture Scrub**, DEV Force ON, **mower round**: tap grass to start an actual mower pass and hear its sound, lock mid-pass, unlock and perform the Zoo escape and LOCK GATE. The exact mower pass should continue from its frozen clock, finish, and **grass taps must respond again**; if the original RAF was genuinely lost, the pass may be safely retried without new grass or score credit. Menu/Keep Scrubbing must still behave.
3. Repeat both with DEV Force OFF (plain Welcome Back), lock once **before** narration/mower and once during it; verify previously working mode. Retry a second lock during cue, escape and Gate; no stale audio callback may thaw, award or resume a silent native mixer. Check denied Apple Music/YouTube PiP/AirPods return, mute, exit and session refusal—all require a fresh real tap.
4. Quick regression: **Traffic Tap, Bible Bugs, Verse Snake**, and Verse Crawl/Balloons/Fish remain working; **Verse Jam** stays excluded on both DEV and Web. Normal native production surprise eligibility and Daily rewards/statistics remain unchanged.

**CI evidence:** [GitHub Actions #43673255337](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/actions/runs/43673255337) PASS on source head `a40a5916`: complete Node suite including native UIKit inactive-first cue and exact Typer/Scrub owner VM replay, Linux Swift syntax parse and production Web build. This does NOT compile the real Apple SDK nor prove physical speaker/mower behavior. Recheck final PR head after the documentation update.

**PR #105 retrospective:** Merged as `fe02a513`, CI passed on final head, and owner subsequently retested all previously failing native Typer narration / Scrub mower lock scenarios, with DEV Force ON and OFF, on a real iPhone: **all working fine**. This is hardware evidence for #105 only; the grouped Learn+Feeding change above still requires its own iPhone tests. Keep the fix PR unmerged until owner review and instruction.


## Merged roadmap PR 1 / [PR #104](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/104) — shared standalone/compatible Playground Zoo cue-first recovery (owner found two active-audio regressions)

**Baseline:** [PR #103](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/103) merged at `59cc8f67`. Owner confirms **Balloons and Fish working fine** on a real iPhone, following the successful Verse Crawl cue from [PR #102](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/102). That is evidence for **those three Read scenes only**, not this PR's Swift/standalone/Playground changes. The multi-PR plan (current shared adapter; next Learn + Feeding; optional quiet Read/special case cleanup) is maintained in [PROJECT-HANDOFF.md](PROJECT-HANDOFF.md#zoo-return-audio-transition--pr-roadmap-maintain-and-update-in-every-later-pr).

**In scope (single broad PR):** All **12 standalone games** opt into the shared `BibloZooWelcomeBackReturn.attach` provisional Zoo cue path; eligible **Ghost Writer, Scripture Scrub, Verse Typer, Wheel of Bible** are wired centrally through `VerseGameShell.attachPlaygroundWelcomeBack`. **Verse Jam stays excluded from ALL Zoo surprises, including DEV Force.** Existing native Audio Test *production* `useZooEscape:false` / `nativeOnlyAudio:true` decisions are kept unchanged: that means **native selected escapes are testable via the explicit main DEV Force Pet Escape switch only**, NOT enabled for normal native users in this PR. On Web/PWA/Android the already-enabled random surprise path now uses cue-first verification. Root Flashcards, Learn and Feeding are not opted in merely because they also use Welcome Back visuals.

**Selected-surprise contract:**
1. On the original **Let's go!** gesture the shared Zoo controller plans, but does not commit, a random Break/Escape or DEV forced Escape. If none is selected, the adapter calls the game's existing `onContinue` within the tap, just as before.
2. If selected and unmuted, play the existing `ui_audio/ui_pet_unlock.mp3` as a short sound **without** invoking original `onContinue` first. On native Audio Test use the preexisting `BibloZooNativeSound` / `pilot-unlock` AVAudioPlayer on its one `.soloAmbient` session: call `activateFromGesture({purpose:"ui"})` in the real tap, `preload`/ `play`, and verify actual `player.currentTime` and `isPlaying` through new narrow read-only `getCuePlaybackState({soundId:"pilot-unlock"})`. An accepted native play request or prepared buffer alone is **not** enough. No HTML/AudioContext fallback on native. Web/PWA/Android use `Audio` direct HTML media with real advancing `currentTime`. In muted mode intentionally play nothing, do not seize external audio solely for a cosmetic sound.
3. Only successful cue verification commits the provisional Zoo odds and shows pets. During rescue the activity remains frozen from the original background event; do **not** call its `onPause` again and inadvertently cut off the cue or change checkpoint clocks.
4. At the real pen / **LOCK GATE**, stop/retire cue and invoke the original game's `onContinue` inside that separate real gesture. It owns native PCM preflight/speaker priority, Web Audio clock, scroll/animation, rounds and score. Only owner-verified success dismisses the gate. Failure reopens an explicit Welcome Back. A cue verified but no longer eligible for Zoo surprise (e.g. storage commit denied) must NOT silently unpause the game; show a new Welcome Back instead. Second lock, OS interruption, Exit and destroy invalidate the original token and stop the cue. Mute/offline/normal no-surprise retain previous behavior.

**Automated acceptance:** `node scripts/validate_shared_zoo_audio_cue_transition.js` (opt-ins, original owner paused, cue vs no-surprise paths, native clock validation, no native HTML fallback, mute, activation/play/final gate failures, second lock and commit failure); `node scripts/validate_patch72_1c_whole_app_zoo_escape.js`, `node scripts/validate_random_zoo_surprises.js`, all N6 native per-game/Playground regressions and `node scripts/validate_native_sound_smoke_test.js`. Run GitHub Actions full Node suite, Linux Swift compiler grammar/syntax and production Web build on **final PR HEAD**; these are not Xcode SDK or audible iPhone tests. Check cache version/offline script resources; the original `ui_pet_unlock.mp3` and native sound resources are already shipped, no new audio asset added.

**Focused real Audio Test iPhone checklist (representative grouped audition rather than rebuilding for 12 individual PRs):**
1. With **DEV Force Pet Escape ON**, test **Traffic Tap, Bible Bugs, Verse Snake** (different game native PCM and timer owners). In each: start active game, play a sound, lock/unlock, tap **Let's go!**. Expect the *BibloPet unlock sound*, not a clipped game sound. Game clock/touches/score remain frozen under escaping pet. At final **LOCK GATE**, original game prepares/reclaims its native PCM and resumes the exact existing game checkpoint.
2. Test **Verse Typer, Scripture Scrub, and one of Ghost Writer / Wheel of Bible** using DEV Force ON in native Audio Test. Preserve typed words, held drawing/tool state, speech/music timing and wheel motion; same cue-first then original owner recovery at Gate. **Verse Jam** must remain ordinary Welcome Back with no Zoo surprise EVEN with DEV Force ON.
3. Turn DEV Force OFF: the same native-only games still use **plain verified Welcome Back** (no new pet or cue). Test a browser/PWA random selected Zoo Break/Escape in one standalone and one compatible Playground game, plus a no-surprise return: cue only for a selected surprise; normal audio recovery remains unchanged.
4. Lock again **while the cue starts**, **during pet rescue**, and **during final LOCK GATE**; with Apple Music/YouTube PiP/AirPods competing, a denied/late native session must not show a pet, consume odds, fall back to Web audio, fake successful game audio, skip scores or auto-resume. A fresh explicit tap is mandatory. Muted must produce no unlock cue and no unauthorized external-audio takeover.
5. Representative **Web/PWA offline** selected return must load the already bundled unlock asset. Spot-check the other standalone screens through the automated registry and a few short sessions rather than 12 separate native builds. Confirm Daily To-Do, Feeding, Learn, Flashcards and the existing **Read Crawl/Balloons/Fish** are unchanged. Do not treat a single iPhone hearing test as blanket verification for every OS route.

**PR #104 retrospective:** complete Node + Linux Swift grammar + production Web CI passed on merged head; owner device test confirmed most games but found Typer-narration and Scrub-mower native interruption regressions described above. This is *partial real-device validation*, not full acceptance. The corrective follow-up needs its own CI and iPhone retest. No native audio category/mode, microphone/recording, downloaded sound, wallet/stats/reward or user-data changes.

## Merged PR #102 — Verse Crawl Zoo Escape cue (owner reports real iPhone success for the audible transition)

**Owner evidence (2026-11-14 local / 2026-11-15 UTC):** [PR #101](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/101) was merged into `experiment/native-ios-audio` at `cc4817731`, and the owner reports **“It's working”** on iPhone. Verse Crawl still emits a short piece of soundtrack *before* the Pet Escape is chosen. Faster parallel clock checks cannot eliminate that audible proof interval. Owner approved an additional PR to replace/mask the early sound with the familiar BibloPet unlock sound, without disturbing the well-tested game freezes or normal audio recovery.

**Source plan:** `fix/crawl-zoo-escape-cue-verification` based on merged #101 `cc4817731`, target ONLY `experiment/native-ios-audio`. For the **Verse Crawl activity alone**, preselect a potential random Zoo Break/Pet Escape (or DEV forced Pet Escape) on the actual Let's go! gesture, without writing stored odds, counting a Break, or showing a pet until the owner returns verified success. If no surprise is selected, recover the original Crawl soundtrack/narration on Let's go! as before. If surprise is selected and sound is enabled, play `./ui_audio/ui_pet_unlock.mp3` as the **only** audible clip in the first recovery gesture. On native Audio Test iOS, the existing N7f Read AVAudioPlayer plugin strictly allowlists `zoo-escape-cue`; on other platforms use original HTML media output. Do NOT call a second iOS sound plugin or add a native fallback. Verify the cue's advancing clip clock or genuine end and native session acceptance—not merely a play() ACK. Once proved, commit ordinary odds, present the rescued pet/break and keep Crawl's words, music and narration frozen. At the final real LOCK GATE tap, stop any cue, use the original active Read owner gesture, restart the original Crawl soundtrack and any narration and verify both real advancing clocks **before** thawing/hiding overlay. If final verification fails, show a retryable ordinary Welcome Back; stale second interruption never thaws gameplay. DEV force has no permanent surprise stats or normal odds changes. Other activities and Zoo To-Do remain unchanged.

**Focused automated checks to review:** `node scripts/validate_random_zoo_surprises.js` (dry roll/no odds write on failed cue, commit only after success, DEV isolation); `node scripts/validate_read_my_verse_welcome_back_patch70.js` (existing real Read owner VM, cue-only first gesture, soundtrack-only final gesture, no-surprise path, failed cue/final music, second interruption); `node scripts/validate_native_n7f_read_my_verse_output.js` (strict native allowlist); previous `node scripts/validate_native_read_external_audio_n7m.js`, `node scripts/validate_native_n7g_integrated_audio_lifecycle.js`, `node scripts/validate_patch72_1c_whole_app_zoo_escape.js`; full repository CI Node suites, Linux Swift syntax parse and production Web build. Record their actual pass/fail on final head, never infer results from source assertions.

**Owner Audio Test iPhone test matrix (after reviewing the PR and syncing the separate Mac Audio Test worktree):**
1. Turn on main DEV Tools **Force Pet Escape**. In **Read My Verse → Verse Crawl**, let music/words play; lock/unlock and tap **Let's go!**. Listen carefully: the brief pre-pet audio should now be the familiar **BibloPet unlock cue**, not a cutoff of the original Crawl soundtrack. Keep music, narration, scrolling and completion frozen throughout rescue.
2. Rescue the pet, tap **LOCK GATE** once. Original Crawl music/narration must restart only at this real tap, pass actual native media verification, then resume at the original scene/verse time. No extra clip, restart from the first verse chunk, duplicate completion or unexpected reward.
3. DEV Force OFF: repeat naturally occurring Zoo Break/Pet Escape in Crawl (first Break odds are high) and a no-surprise audio return. Only selected surprises get the cue; a normal return should sound exactly as it did before, without unlocking audio or manipulating odds beyond the original random policy. Also test app muted (no surprise sound).
4. While external YouTube PiP/Apple Music/AirPods audio has priority, try Let's go; a denied/deferred native session must not show a pet, count Zoo odds, claim success or play through WebKit fallback. Retry requires a new genuine tap. Lock again during the first cue, during pet rescue, and during the final Lock Gate recovery; each stale verification must fail closed and never thaw Crawl.
5. Confirm **Balloons/Fish/other Read activities**, standalone **Traffic Tap** Lock Gate, **Verse Jam** no-surprise exemption including DEV force ON, Daily Zoo To-Do and permanent Zookeeper stats behave exactly as in merged PR #101.

**Evidence and safety (PR #102 retrospective):** Node regressions, Linux Swift parse and Web build passed on PR #102; the owner subsequently auditioned the Verse Crawl cue on actual Audio Test iPhone and reports it seemed to work. That acceptance does **not** establish Balloons/Fish results, an Apple SDK build in Linux CI, or blanket PiP/AirPods proof. No mic/recording/category/privacy/reward changes.


## Merged PR #101 — Zoo transition polish and Verse Jam exclusion (owner reports iPhone working; Crawl cue follow-up above)

**Baseline and evidence:** [PR #100](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/100) merged into `experiment/native-ios-audio` at `fe2cdb12` (2026-11-15 UTC). The owner extensively tested the actual Audio Test app and reports core activity freeze/rescue working as intended. Remaining observations: short audible Verse Crawl start before Pet Escape, delayed Verse Jam Zoo return and initially unresponsive-looking LOCK GATE, and occasional normal Welcome Back flashing after LOCK GATE (notably Traffic Tap and Verse Jam). This follow-up is deliberately smaller than optimizing Verse Jam's entire native PCM bank.

**Approved changes:**
- **Verse Jam** (on all platforms) always uses **ordinary Welcome Back → verified Let's go! → original Verse Jam**. No random Zoo Break or Pet Escape. The native Audio Test **Force Pet Escape ON** must not override this exemption. Other Playground/standalone games and actual Daily To-Do surprises retain their former behavior and random odds.
- **Shared completed Zoo rescue:** Immediately disable the final button and show **Opening the gate... / Getting your game ready...** in the same Zoo visual. Keep that presentation while the activity owner checks/resumes native audio asynchronously; do not reset to ordinary Welcome Back between a successful LOCK GATE tap and dismissal. If iOS denies/cancels audio, the original owner must still return the child to a fresh explicitly tapped Welcome Back (no hidden auto-resume). Repeated taps cannot run reward/continue callbacks twice. Zoo rescue and statistics remain single-award.
- **Read My Verse Verse Crawl:** StartPromise checks for soundtrack and narration run in parallel, then check both **real advancing audio clocks in parallel** every 95ms (up to 14 samples, same >35ms threshold). The audio burst before a chosen pet escape should be shorter, but the app does **not** silence playback, fake output verification, auto-reclaim on foreground, or thaw scene/verse timers early. A brief detectable lead-in may remain; judge by real iPhone audition.

**Automated checks (run/verify on PR head):** `node scripts/validate_random_zoo_surprises.js`; `node scripts/validate_patch72_1c_whole_app_zoo_escape.js`; `node scripts/validate_read_my_verse_welcome_back_patch70.js`; `node scripts/validate_native_playground_verse_jam_n6q.js`; `node scripts/validate_native_traffic_tap_n6l.js`; plus all repository CI Node suites and `npm run build`. Linux JS source tests, Swift grammar parsing and Web builds cannot prove native Apple SDK compilation, speaker output or touch responsiveness on an actual iPhone.

**Owner's actual iPhone checklist after checking out/merging this follow-up into dedicated Audio Test worktree:** Pull origin; `npm run build && npx cap sync ios && npx cap open ios`, Xcode run target `com.andydoane.biblozoo.audiotest`.

1. **Verse Jam with DEV force OFF and ON:** During running phrases, lock/unlock, press Let's go. Expect one standard Welcome Back recovery, then the same Verse Jam timing/score, **never** a Pet Escape, Zoo Break, pen or LOCK GATE. Also confirm native iPhone audio actually plays and retry/Back still work if a foreign session denies activation. Web/PWA/Android are likewise excluded.
2. **Verse Crawl with DEV force ON:** Lock while Crawl music/words are active, unlock and press Let's go. Listen for whether the pre-escape audio burst is **materially shorter** than PR #100; do not claim it eliminated without hearing. Crawl music/narration and animations must stay frozen during the pet escape. After rescue tap LOCK GATE once, verify playback/scene continue correctly from original checkpoint with no duplicate narration or rewards.
3. **Traffic Tap and other Zoo-enabled standalone/Playground modes:** Trigger forced escape, capture pet and press LOCK GATE. Verify it instantly displays **Opening the gate...** in the Zoo overlay, not the ordinary Welcome Back popup. When native audio is confirmed, overlay vanishes directly to the activity. Pressing again must not duplicate resume or scoring.
4. **Slow native recovery:** During a slow Lock Gate, the gate stays visible with progress text and is not interactive again; original activity remains held. A genuine denial/deferred result returns to a retryable explicit **Welcome Back**, never resumes behind the overlay. Test a second lock/unlock during a pet rescue or while Lock Gate is still recovering, and confirm old success cannot thaw the new interruption.
5. **Privacy, statistics and compatibility:** DEV forced rescues never increase permanent pet/break stats or daily odds. No changes to mic capture, audio categories, Feeding reward claims, profiles, local backups, Web/PWA/Android non-Jam gameplay, or DEV toggle behavior for other activities.

**Acceptance status:** Implementation in feature PR only; automated CI and real Xcode/physical iPhone results must be recorded separately. Owner test **PENDING**.


## PR #100 — Zoo Escape underlay pause and DEV Force Pet Escape (MERGED; core owner iPhone PASS, follow-up above)

**Owner-reproduced, 2026-11-14:** In actual Audio Test iPhone, start Read My Verse **Verse Crawl**, lock/unlock, tap **Let's go!**, let a random **Pet Escape** occur; pet drag/pen rescue was possible while the *underlying Crawl was moving/playing music*. This is NOT an intentional overlapping scene. Verified source ordering: activity audio recovery releases its timers/animations first; shared Zoo Surprise selects Pet Escape only afterwards. The correction needs a TWO-PHASE continuation: sound verified on Let's go, gameplay only resumes after a no-surprise outcome or the child's final LOCK GATE click.

**Exact change scope on feature branch `fix/zoo-escape-holds-activity-dev-force-test`:**
- All **eight Read My Verse** activities share one retained `readAudioRecovery` scene guard until the Zoo controller's `onRecovered` callback: Verse Crawl, Balloons, Fish, Typewriter, Keyboard, Unscramble, Tap Words in Order, Chunk Audio Matching. No music/animated timeline/timer/verse progression or completion credit while a pet is out. On verified surprise start, pause actually playing Crawl soundtrack/ending and spoken narration; resume these from the final real pen/LOCK GATE gesture, then thaw exactly the original animation timeline and delayed callbacks. If locked again during rescue, invalidate the original audio proof and demand a new Welcome Back instead of unfreezing on an obsolete attempt.
- Shared **12 standalone verse games**, **five Playground games**: `onSurpriseStart` calls existing owner-owned pause after initial audio recovery; `onRecovered` calls that original owner-controlled audio/clock recovery AGAIN from the pen/LOCK GATE gesture, keeping the modal up until it resolves. No synthetic success, no auto-advance during rescue and no loss of original state. Audio denial/foreign PiP remains retryable. Native-only activities with previously approved plain return are unchanged **when DEV force is OFF**.
- **Learn Listen/Chunks/Echo** and the **four Feeding games**: reassert owner pause when a surprise starts, then release the existing in-memory checkpoint/rally only on final LOCK GATE. No new reward path, starts, microphone, recording, AVFoundation, new Swift audio session category or changed WebAudio pipeline.
- Main-screen native Audio Test **DEV Tools** drawer: new **Force Pet Escape on Let's go!: OFF/ON** button (persisted per app installation). Enable it, lock the iPhone during a supported active game or audio phase, unlock, and tap **Let's go!**. **Every successful verified audio return** should open one regular Pet Escape immediately, rather than random Zoo Break/none. A real audio failure/deferred `.soloAmbient` takeover must not trigger one. For native-only game families that normally opt out of Zoo Escape, the switch is a **DEV-only opt-in**, not a new production behavior.
- **DEV force does not write the ordinary Zoo surprise odds/day record or award permanent Pets Rescued/Zoo Breaks Stopped stats**, and does not affect real Daily Zoo To-Do, snacks, medals, personal records or lifetime game achievements. Toggle back OFF when done; normal odds and native plain returns resume. This test switch is unavailable on public Web/PWA/Android, hidden from child interface, and does not bypass audio verification.

**Automated regression:** Updated existing real-source Node VM tests `scripts/validate_read_my_verse_welcome_back_patch70.js` (quiet Read timers remain frozen, Balloons CSS animations frozen, actual Crawl HTML music paused during pets and resumed from final lock); `scripts/validate_patch72_1c_whole_app_zoo_escape.js` (common owner second pause and verified second gesture); `scripts/validate_random_zoo_surprises.js` (DEV native-only force, no changed production odds/state), and `scripts/validate_patch72_1b_feeding_zoo_escape.js` (native test opt-in). All existing N7m silent-phase audio, N7g, N7f.2 and native Swift parse/Web build jobs must remain green. **[PR #100](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/100) corrected code/test head `6c4da650f6f0e64941ab9e1f80fd0b9a0d420d13` Actions [#43659578372](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/actions/runs/43659578372) PASS** full Node regressions including the new Read/Crawl/shared Zoo logic and DEV force choices, Linux Swift syntax parse and production Web build. This evidence-only docs commit triggers a final-head recheck. At merge, Mac Xcode/Apple SDK compilation was not established by CI. The owner **subsequently tested PR #100 extensively on a real iPhone**, confirms the fundamental freeze/rescue behavior works, and identified the transition issues documented in the new follow-up section above.

**Owner iPhone acceptance matrix — only after owner merges PR into `experiment/native-ios-audio`:** In a dedicated Mac Audio Test worktree Pull Origin, `npm run build`, `npx cap sync ios`, `npx cap open ios`, run `com.andydoane.biblozoo.audiotest` in Xcode on real iPhone.

## PR #99 — Feeding four results, Bounce full clear, and Labyrinth ten-second rescue (owner iPhone PENDING)

**Owner-approved 2026-11-14 UI scope:** Real Daily To-Do **AND hidden DEV Feeding Game Tester** must show the *same* end-screen design for Classic, Labyrinth, Bounce, Melody. Every end card shows a solid **Zookeeper Stats green `#24783c` and white** **✔ To-Do Complete!** button (same actual back navigation; debug still exits to Zoo); remove the separate purple `Zoo To-Do Complete!` line everywhere. Food summary reads exactly `Caught X Snacks` with NO `of X` denominator; only actually caught food pieces count. Melody's yellow score pill displays **`dq_feed_music_note.png` × X%** (real musical-note catch percentage, no fake emoji and no food/note conflation), preserving original percentages and underlying game score, pitches and timing. Result header is shared between actual and DEV endings. The preview *looks* complete, but never earns a Daily claim, Snacks Eaten, badges, streak, rotation turn or unrelated persistent progress. No rework of secret debug menu/launcher.

**Bounce authoritatively approved rules:** There are **20 bricks, seven snack-bearing** and up to two earned star bricks, with up to **three live white balls**. Before breaking the seventh snack brick, original three-launch behavior applies. Breaking the **seventh** colored snack brick starts the **last rally**: keep every currently on-screen white ball moving; if all go below paddle, game ends without further launches even if unused launches remain. If **all 20 bricks** are destroyed before the last live white ball is missed, queue one one-time bonus shower based ONLY on **active on-screen white balls at final brick impact**: **one ball → 2–3**, **two balls → 4–5**, **three balls → 6–7** actual BibloSnacks. Pending/unused star white balls and spare serves are not counted. Bonus uses each of six solid colors at most once, rainbow seventh only when seven, left/right randomized safe lanes and **450ms** gaps. On full clear, white balls stop bouncing and staggered bonus food is catchable; wait for all queued and falling food to be caught or missed before results. One Rainbow = **one** snack, **five** score points. Up to 7 original + 7 bonus (=14 food max) remain under the existing real claim max 16; only real successfully claimed Daily feeding increments lifetime `totalSnacksEaten`, no duplicate claim or preview credit. Preserve normal 900ms finish wait, food impact sounds, scoring/rewards and original control methods. On app switch/Welcome Back, pause and shift pending bonus deadlines as with other Feeding timelines; never burst overdue rewards immediately on return.

**Labyrinth stuck-ball rescue:** Ten seconds (`LABYRINTH_STUCK_MS=10000`) of insufficient movement replaces the original 3500ms timer, and the assist only applies if the ball is supported atop an actual solid platform. A ball in freefall cannot be nudged even after a stale timer. The no-input child is allowed to see a stationary food piece while deciding to drag; the last-resort gap-directed kick remains to prevent an indefinitely unfinishable board. No changes to drag/tilt input gains, gravity, floating BibloPet or the seven-food scoring.

**Implementation:** Scoped `daily_questions_feed_game.js`, `daily_questions_feed_game.css`, updated `scripts/validate_daily_feed_game.js` in `feature/feed-results-bounce-full-clear-labyrinth-rescue` based on native experiment `d05d7c35db18396997de568f2fb15a56213297a2`, plus this test guide and the mandatory living handoff. The scripted VM tests now cover previews matching actual results for all four modes, dark-green/white pill, note artwork/percentage, Labyrinth no early/airborne nudge and eventual platform release, old Bounce balls preserved on seventh snack, no spare launch in last rally, 1/2/3-active-ball ranges at low and high deterministic randomness, unique food colors and seventh Rainbow, 450ms stagger, caught-once and 900ms finish, no premature result and preview non-credit. Prior VM/static validations for Native-only pilot, interruptions and distinct food vs musical notes remain.

**Owner physical Audio Test iPhone acceptance after merge only into `experiment/native-ios-audio`:** In the dedicated Mac Audio Test worktree Pull Origin → `npm run build && npx cap sync ios && npx cap open ios`; build and run `com.andydoane.biblozoo.audiotest` in Xcode on a real iPhone. These steps do NOT apply to `ios-prep` or `main`.

1. **Results parity:** Secret DEV Tools → Feeding Game Tester → Classic / Labyrinth / Bounce / Melody, finish each, record screenshot. Then complete ordinary Daily To-Do to unlock each mode on different assigned days and compare ending screenshot against the DEV mode. Expect identical art/title/catch count/design: **✔ To-Do Complete!** button in exact dark Zookeeper Stats green with **white** text, no extra purple line, and `Caught X Snacks`. Melody shows its **real music note PNG** followed by `× X%` using note-catch percentage. Preview's displayed `To-Do Complete!` does NOT award achievements; real valid Daily claim still awards once.
2. **Bounce rally:** In DEV preview, break all seven colored snack bricks while some gray bricks remain; observe white ball(s) **keep playing**. Miss all live white balls without finishing gray bricks: **no next “Balls Left!” launch** even with spare serves; any already falling food completes its descent, then results.
3. **Bounce bonus:** Clear all 20 bricks with 1 / 2 / 3 visibly on-screen white balls (star bricks spawn up to two extra). Expect randomized **2–3 / 4–5 / 6–7**, respectively; no duplicate color and exactly one rainbow only if count 7. Shower alternates left/right with catchable stagger, existing BibloPet/paddle food-catch reactions and chomp. Rainbow adds five score but exactly one eaten food. No early results while a drop is still on-screen or scheduled, no second shower, no soft-lock after the board is empty.
4. **Pause/return:** Background iPhone during the white-ball final rally or bonus-food shower, then return via existing real gesture/Welcome Back. Confirm no automated resume/foreign speaker takeover, no instant burst of all bonus snacks or loss of reward state, normal native collision/chomp sounds after recovery. Earlier PR #98 Zookeeper portrait in next-ball popup and original Tilt/Drag control art remain unchanged.
5. **Labyrinth:** With **Drag** selected, let a snack rest naturally on a platform and do not touch the screen. Confirm **no 3.5-second sideways self-play**; if it really stays stuck 10s, expect one gap-seeking rescue. Free-falling balls never get pushed. Continue normal drag/tilt to finish all seven, test across portrait/landscape, check chomp and unmodified rewards.
6. **Persistent stats:** Repeat previews and replay results; verify the tester **never adds lifetime Snacks Eaten, badges, Daily completion or rotation**, while a real current-day completed To-Do claim credits only the actually caught colored food once, including caught Bounce bonus. Verify no unclaimed old-day carryover per merged PR #97.
7. **Native boundaries:** Do not infer physical Apple SDK compile/audio PASS from Node/Linux Swift grammar or Web build. No intentional Swift/AVFoundation/`.soloAmbient` changes, no recording/mic/Photos permission change, no WebAudio fallback, and no unrelated Read/Flashcards/Welcome Back changes.

## Daily Zoo To-Do strict calendar-day reset (owner iPhone PENDING)

**Owner-reported reproduction and explicit choice (2026-11-14):** A child finished two of the ordinary Daily Tasks on one local date and the third on a subsequent calendar date. The old checklist showed three checkmarks and enabled "Feed me a snack," but tapping it showed **Snack Unavailable — Confirm all three Daily Tasks**. After closing/reopening the app, a clean new Daily To-Do appeared. Source-level cause: an in-memory old CARE plan could remain visible/complete, while Feeding validates `plan.day === localDayKey(now)`. The owner chooses **a fresh ordinary three-task checklist each local calendar day, no carryover of unfinished Daily tasks or unclaimed previous-day feeding**. Do not imply that the screenshot establishes which exact internal callback fired.

**[PR #97](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/97) scope:** Only feature branch `fix/daily-todo-strict-calendar-day-rollover` targeting `experiment/native-ios-audio`, base `f3682a5` (merged #96). In `daily_zoo_todo.js`, CARE plans always expire on a date mismatch even if `rolloverHold` is active, and late `beginTask`/`setPendingCompletion`/`confirmPendingCompletion` cannot change old Daily state or mint task totals/streak/snack. In `app.js`, schedule the next **local** midnight (not UTC fixed 24h) and recheck on iOS foreground/pageshow/focus, rerender the home/TODO screen to create today's plan; stale-row tap also replaces the plan and shows **A New Day at the Zoo!** rather than inaccurate "finish all three" copy. Keep the final `markSnackClaimed` old-day and exactly-once guard. A child already playing during midnight may finish the separate game/verse activity, but cannot collect yesterday's Daily checklist completion/snack. An unfinished **NEW_PET mission** has its existing deliberate cross-day preservation, not a standard Daily checklist. No erase/rollback of confirmed historical task totals/earned badges, lifetime Snacks Eaten, pet/verse progress, or recorded microphone takes; no Swift/AVAudioSession or native/WKWebView audio recovery changes. Web/PWA uses the same calendar-day checklist rules, its original sound playback remains unchanged.

**Automated regression:** `npm run validate:daily-zoo-todo` now tests a running CARE task at midnight, two completed prior-day tasks with a late third confirmation rejected, stale begin/pending/claim refusal, fresh today checklist and preserved lifetime task stats/verse progress. It also checks the app's day-boundary schedule/return handlers and stale-tap feedback. GitHub Actions experimental PR workflow runs this plus the full existing JS regression selection, whole-file Swift syntax parsing (not Apple SDK type-check), and `npm run build`. **PR #97 initial Node/Swift grammar/Web build GitHub Actions PASS** on `b42a528adc08d33b538b2c3a43c7564b813bb5b5` ([run #43629823823](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/actions/runs/43629823823)); final PR-head Node/Swift-parse/Web GitHub Actions also PASS ([run #43629926712](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/actions/runs/43629926712)); PR #97 merged at `ebfe1c1`, owner provisionally accepted, while actual overnight iPhone reproduction remains pending.**

Prefer simulated local date advancement on a test device/profile rather than waiting across days; restore automatic time afterward.

Repeat while the app is backgrounded/locked and on foreground return without force-quitting.

The underlying game/verse progress may still save normally, but the old Daily completion, streak and feeding reward must **not** be awarded. Return to Zoo To-Do and see today's fresh tasks, with no misleading "Confirm all three" error.
3. Finish all **three on Day 1** but **do not feed**; cross midnight and verify Day 1 snack expires. Finish all three tasks on Day 2 and feed on the same day: exactly one normal snack award/claim, original Feeding modes and native sounds; no double-credit on reentry.
4. Confirm historical **Tasks Completed**, Task/Streak badges, lifetime Snacks Eaten, BibloPets, verse progress and saved medals remain after rollover. Confirm special unfinished **New BibloPet mission** can still continue across dates, including its activity/game and package.

## PR #96 — Melody: seven actual snacks, lifetime Snacks Eaten, owner-supplied animated badges (owner iPhone PENDING)

**Owner request and source art:** Owner has uploaded `verse_images/daily_questions/dq_feed_music_note.png` and the following **actual supplied** files in `verse_images/daily_zoo_todo/`: `snacks_10.png`, `snacks_25.png`, `snacks_50.png`, `snacks_100.png`, `snacks_200.png`, `snacks_300.png`, `snacks_400.png`, `snacks_500.png`, `snacks_600.png`, `snacks_700.png`, `snacks_800.png`, `snacks_900.png`, `snacks_1000.gif.gif`. The last name has **two .gif suffixes in GitHub**, but is the owner's special *animated* 1,000-Snacks badge; the code points to that exact real filename rather than guessing `.gif`. The owner supplied 200–900 graphics instead of the previously discussed conceptual 250 and 2000 tiers; implementation intentionally follows the **real artwork**, starting from thresholds **10,25,50,100,200,300,400,500,600,700,800,900,1000**.

**Melody gameplay contract:** All nine approved melodies retain their actual original music note arrays, rests, BPM, MIDI pitches, arrival/beat offsets and horizontal lane paths; the existing 35–83 playable note events and the exact original `Caught %` denominator/score **do not change**. A new `assignMelodySnacks(timeline)` picks **exactly seven** playable note entries spaced across actual **musical arrival times**, not equally along array positions, and assigns one each of the existing six solid color BibloSnacks and rainbow. Each of the other notes renders the new `dq_feed_music_note.png` at unchanged size, trajectory and beat contact. Each note and each snack still plays its original MIDI pitch through the **same** existing `playMelodyTone(runtime, entry.midi)` when caught. Ordinary notes still increase `caughtCount` for music percentage, **never** `snacksCaught` or food stats; only an entry with `snackConfig` may increment `snacksCaught`, only once per physical catch. Food shows a +1 visual; ordinary music notes do not falsely display an edible score. No new chomp, audio session, oscillator or sound interaction was introduced.

**Feeding result contract:** Real Melody result keeps `catchPercent` derived from `caughtCount / totalCount`; it also returns the independent `snacksCaught` and `snacksTotal=7`. The top-right `Caught %` remains unchanged. End screen shows **`Music Notes Caught: 84%`** (for example) and **`Caught 5 of 7 snacks`** separately, instead of hiding the number-of-snacks line and incorrectly labeling percentage as “Snacks Caught.” Existing Classic/Labyrinth/Bounce `Score` and `Caught X of 7 snacks` remain unchanged; their usual `caughtCount` counts actual eaten pieces (not points, so a five-point rainbow is only one snack). Classic can release extra food when star pegs fire (up to 16 total).

**Zookeeper lifetime persistence / anti-farming:** The existing per-Zookeeper, on-device `dailyZooTodo.stats` record gains `totalSnacksEaten` (nonnegative integer, defaults to zero for historical accounts), and `earnedSnackBadges` (persisted unlocked thresholds). **Only** a completed, correctly owned, current-day, *actual*, non-preview Daily feeding game's `onComplete` supplies food to `claimDailyTodoSnack()`; that function calls `markSnackClaimed` with Melody's `snacksCaught`, but for the other three modes with the original actual `caughtCount`. The established `plan.snack.claimed` guard and authoritative `saveDailyTodoRuntime` persist food **atomically** with the claim, so repeated callbacks, game reopen and screen reload cannot add the same snacks twice; previews never reach this real-claim path and early Back/abandonment does not award snacks. A validated round adds at most 16 snacks; no old past catches are guessed or backfilled. A child with a second Zookeeper profile has separate stats. Ordinary regular note catches and decorative points never contribute to food. Completed medals and other reward stats unchanged.

**Badges:** `SNACK_BADGE_THRESHOLDS` and `BADGE_DEFINITIONS.snacks` are in `daily_zoo_todo.js`. Existing persisted badge normalization and `getEarnedBadgeDefinitions` handle Snack badges beside Streak/Task without changing either old category or existing achievement. `app.js` shows the lifetime **Snacks Eaten** count and a new **Snack Badges** group using the existing `zookeeperBadgeGroupHtml()` image renderer, which supports both PNG and animated GIF through normal `<img src>`. The special 1,000 GIF uses the actual `snacks_1000.gif.gif` URL. A profile's existing past tasks/streaks are preserved when the new `stats` fields normalize.

**Offline:** Service worker cache version updated to `2026_Nov_14_patch73_melody_seven_snacks_badges`; new note image and all 13 uploaded badge files are cached in the existing **optional** Feeding art cache (individual failures do **not** block essential shell installation). Physical native Capacitor bundle uses the same local image assets. Existing N7 native-only sound and interruption pathways are untouched.

**Automated regression:** `node scripts/validate_daily-feed-game.js` checks all nine tunes and exact seven food slots, increasing musical arrival times, untouched original pitch/beat/lane/rest timeline, separately caught notes vs snacks, duplicate catches, original percentage, visible end screen counts, and unmodified native melody owner audio. `node scripts/validate_daily-zoo-todo.js` checks uploaded asset existence including special double-GIF, every Snack badge threshold, legacy normalized zero, real completed claimed reward increment, reload, duplicate claim protection, invalid input clamp and no uncompleted game credit. `node scripts/validate_patch73_1_feeding_polish.js` checks updated cache string and both note/GIF offline assets. Existing comprehensive GitHub Actions Node, Linux Swift syntax parse and Web build are mandatory. **A green CI is NOT an Apple SDK/Xcode build or physical iPhone evidence.**

Confirm **Snacks Eaten** increases by X, not total notes, and re-entry/repeated completed result never awards twice. Over successive days test Classic star bonus catches (rainbow=one food), Labyrinth, Bounce, crossing 10/25 Snack Badge thresholds and separate Zookeepers; special 1,000 GIF is expected animated when eligible, but may require a developer seed rather than actual 1,000 catches for owner testing. ## Repeatable Zoo Break odds: first of day 55%, later 5%/2%; Pet Escape after non-break (owner iPhone PENDING)

**Why this follow-up:** Owner merged PR #94 into `experiment/native-ios-audio` at `fcc0e58d6171d341cd66a9033cfc9ec742028dd3`. In device exploration, the owner repeatedly tried to trigger a Zoo Break without success; PR #94 allowed only one 25% Daily chance with a 20% Break split (an actual 5% Break chance), and one 10% Welcome chance with a 20% Break split (an actual 2% Break chance). Even repeated tapping did not re-roll. Owner explicitly approves removing all once-per-day and single-roll restrictions and wants **at least a 50% chance to encounter a first Zoo Break at an eligible trigger each day**, followed by low-but-nonzero chances of a second or third that day. No new per-session clock tracking or artificial audio recovery.

**Revised policy:** Every eligible **Daily To-Do “I'm Ready!”**, **wide home Zoo To-Do button**, and **successfully verified Welcome Back → Let's go!** rolls again; neither a miss nor a Pet Escape exhausts the chance. For **each Zookeeper**, until the first **randomly selected** Zoo Break of their **local calendar day**, the next eligible trigger has a **55% chance of a full Zoo Break**, whether it is Zoo To-Do or Welcome Back. After the first selected Break, later Zoo To-Do presses independently have a **5% Break chance** and later **verified Welcome Back** clicks have a **2% Break chance**. If no Break is selected, roll **15% chance for a one-pet Escape**; otherwise continue unchanged. A Pet Escape doesn't lower future Break odds. Repeatable rolls allow second, third, etc. Breaks. Consequences: before the first Break, outcomes per eligible press are **55% Break / 6.75% Pet Escape / 38.25% nothing**; after it, Zoo To-Do outcomes are **5% Break / 14.25% Pet / 80.75% nothing**, while verified Welcome Back is **2% Break / 14.7% Pet / 83.3% nothing**. Probabilities are independent at each eligible tap, without a cooldown, but the UI still prevents overlapping modals. **This is not an automatic 55% chance for an app session in which the child never taps an eligible button.**

**Persistent state:** `BibloZooZooSurprise.choose(profileId,source)` in `verse_games/shared/welcome-back-zoo-escape.js` tracks only `{day,breaksTriggered}` under per-profile `biblozooZooSurprise:v2:<profileId>`. A selected full Zoo Break immediately increases `breaksTriggered`, **even if the child later quits before LOCK GATE**; this prevents skipping a Break to repeatedly obtain the initial 55% odds. Pet Escape, miss, and actual pet rescue statistics do **not** alter this eligibility counter. A new local date resets to zero; different profiles have independent counters. Retired `v1` `dailyTried/welcomeTried/shown` flags no longer suppress events, so upgrading from #94 grants fresh eligibility. `profiles.js` removes both v1 and v2 chance keys when deleting a Zookeeper with progress. If the profile or localStorage is unavailable, skip the cosmetic surprise and preserve intended task navigation.

**Unchanged audio contract:** A true Welcome Back / Let's go! gesture still invokes the activity's original `recover()` / `onContinue()` **synchronously** in its exact real click, so native iOS retains the gesture-bound `AVAudioSession .soloAmbient` activation/foreground/interruption rules. The entertainment chance is checked **only after a verified `ok === true`**, never on deferred/OS takeover/timeout/rejection/failure. Failed recovery retains the existing explicit retry/back safety. A recreational LOCK GATE must **never** invoke `recover()` a second time or fake media output/recording/score. Existing native-only plain-popup opt-outs remain intact, so these locations do not suddenly grow unrelated random audio behavior. No changes to Swift plugins, native audio claims, microphone, asset playback, game progress or Zookeeper rescues/stopped-Break stats (the latter still require real catches and gate completion).

**Automated validation:** `node scripts/validate_random_zoo_surprises.js` updated to cover a miss followed by another eligible tap, 55% first Break from either trigger, daily reset, Pet before first Break with unaltered odds, per-Zookeeper independence, repeated low-chance second and third Breaks, retired v1 suppression ignored, separate Pet on non-break, and unusable storage fail-closed. It also runs the actual prior rescue FakeDOM/LOCK GATE, verifies captured pet and stopped-break counters, and asserts original Let's go! recovery runs before surprise while true failure/deferred native interruption never selects one. All original Patch 72 sound recovery, N7 native OS interruption, Web build and Swift syntax CI checks remain. **Physical Apple SDK build and audible iPhone/UI acceptance are not established by CI.**

**Hidden long-press behavior already present:** The **wide Zoo To-Do home button**, long-pressed about **1.2 seconds**, invokes `showDailyPreviewDialog` (Daily To-Do developer preview, *not* a direct Zoo Break). In an active **Zoo-enabled Welcome Back popup**, holding its **title** for **2.4 seconds** can open the existing `Zoo Escape TEST MODE` menu, with **Stage 3 → Verify real recovery / Stage 3 → Zoo Exit**, etc. That developer menu simulates staged recovery failure only and still uses the real original owner on a selected “real recovery” step; it is **not the normal randomized flow**. The feature is intentionally inaccessible while a random rescue is already in progress. Feeding has its separate score badge hold harness. Do not enable more of the preexisting developer harness on public releases.

**Owner iPhone acceptance:** Review/merge follow-up only to `experiment/native-ios-audio`; Pull Origin in isolated Mac worktree → `npm run build && npx cap sync ios && npx cap open ios` → build Xcode **App** on physical `com.andydoane.biblozoo.audiotest`. Use wide Zoo To-Do repeatedly (return Home between attempts); unlike PR #94, **every tap is a new roll**. A first event should often be the full multi-pet Zoo Break; completing gate increments the original `zooBreaksStopped` once, and the next attempts have the lower 5% rate. The popup I'm Ready! is still eligible when present. A Pet Escape can occur without consuming first-Break odds. Test re-entry, cancel/Back, clock rollover, two Zookeepers, preserved Daily plan and original routing. On a **true** Welcome Back, use Let's go! and verify real native audio recovery **first**; under Apple Music/PiP/AirPods interruptions and rejection the random event must **not** appear and no sound/session may be silently reactivated. Verify #93 Flashcards post-mic Listen, Learn/Read/Feeding and other native-only recovery opt-outs still work. Owner real-device evidence **PENDING**.

## PR #94 historical random Zoo odds (superseded by repeatable 55% model below)

**Owner evidence and goal:** PR #93 merged in `experiment/native-ios-audio` at `dc5801d2677b9554b77450cb528ee71f5f65be62`; owner reports first-attempt post-microphone Flashcards Listen narration is now FIXED on a real iPhone. New request: retain the beloved one-pet rescue / 6–10 pet Zoo Break visuals, rescue motions, LOCK GATE, and per-Zookeeper counters, but **do not trigger them from audio recovery failures**. Instead the child may encounter a chance event on a verified successful **Welcome Back → Let's go!**, the Daily Zoo To-Do popup **I'm Ready!**, or the wide home **Zoo To-Do** button.

**Odds and persistence:** At a local calendar-day boundary, each Zookeeper has one 25% `daily` opportunity shared by **both** Zoo To-Do entry buttons (never two rolls if the child returns home), and one independent 10% `welcome` opportunity after successful recovery. If either succeeds, the **same** Zookeeper receives at most one surprise that day. Of chosen surprises, 80% are a single Pet Escape and 20% a full Zoo Break. Probability misses are remembered for that source/day to prevent tapping repeatedly for another roll. State lives in separate per-profile `biblozooZooSurprise:v1:<profileId>` localStorage, never verse progress or personal telemetry. If storage/profile invalid, normal navigation proceeds and no surprise is attempted. Deleting a Zookeeper must also clear this new per-profile preference key (see profile deletion regression). Existing `biblozooZooEscapeStats:v1:<profileId>` continues to count *actual rescued pets* individually, and `zooBreaksStopped` only after a fully rescued break and genuine final LOCK GATE; developer tests do not credit rewards.

**Real audio safety — nonnegotiable:** Existing per-activity `recover()`, `onContinue()`, native `.soloAmbient` activation/foreground and interruption gates stay untouched. The existing Zoo-enabled Welcome Back controller still calls audio recovery **synchronously in the child's actual Let's go! tap**, awaits a verified `true`, and only then checks the 10% fun chance. A deferred `{deferred:true}`, lost foreground, Apple Music/PiP/audio takeover, failure, rejection or timeout must **not** produce a recreational pet. For newly enabled production surprise mode, a normal audio failure keeps the same Welcome Back recovery dialog showing retry and a safe **Back to Zoo** action, up to the existing three-gesture bound; no fake loss automatically spawns pets. The old failure-driven 3-stage path is kept only for existing source regression/developer forced scenarios, whose stats are excluded. Native-only plain recovery popup opt-outs remain unchanged rather than silently routing them through a new audio activation path; the shared Zoo-enabled Web, Read, Flashcards, quiet return and Feeding owners opt into the post-success fun check. No new native sound session, spontaneous WebAudio, microphone invocation, auto retry, reward/score changes or interrupted game state reset.

**Zoo To-Do navigation:** The two buttons call one `openZooTodoWithSurprise(destination)` continuation. On a miss they use the exact old `openDailyTodoPreview(mode)`. On a hit the home Daily offer disappears (if present), the *same existing yellow Welcome Back-style Zoo rescue overlay* opens immediately in `escape` or `break` stage rather than audio-recovery stages, and children use the same tap/drag rescue and LOCK GATE. **No `recover()` call is allowed from a recreational LOCK GATE.** On successful gate or Back to Zoo, dispose overlay/timers and continue the original Zoo To-Do destination (including the wide button's conditional `actual` mode). The child's plan, completed tasks, feeding eligibility and scores remain intact. If the popup cannot be made, fail open to the original navigation rather than trap the child; never stack multiple prompts or invoke a new audio priority claim. Background/foreground preserves the active mini-event using the existing controller's visibility behavior.

**Automated regression:** `node scripts/validate_random_zoo_surprises.js` runs the real shared controller with the previously proven Patch 72 fake DOM, fixed randomness, localStorage and real rescue gesture events: chance/daily rollover/profile isolation, 80/20 split, shared button source, verified recovery BEFORE any event, no event on failed/deferred audio, three bounded retries, no false recovery calls on random LOCK GATE, one per-pet stat and gate-confirmed Zoo Break count, and return callback. The CI suite also runs all existing Patch 72 stage, native audio interruption, game/flashcards/Read, whole app and Web build checks. A green Node/Swift grammar/Web build **does not prove iPhone Xcode compilation, output priority or actual physical user experience**.

**Owner physical iPhone test (experimental Audio Test only):** Review/merge only into `experiment/native-ios-audio`; Pull Origin in the separate Mac worktree, `npm run build && npx cap sync ios && npx cap open ios`, build **App** with `com.andydoane.biblozoo.audiotest`. Check both entry buttons independently; the expected daily roll is shared, so normal repeated taps never give extra chances. With a rare successful roll, see familiar Pet Escape or complete Zoo Break immediately instead of the Zoo To-Do offer/preview; rescue pets and LOCK GATE, then return to the **correct original Zoo To-Do state**; verify Zookeeper stats once. Back/Exit should return to the intended Daily To-Do route; lock/app switch during animation should not duplicate events or lose the plan. On an eligible Welcome Back, confirm **Let's go! first actually resumes/recovers the real native audio owner**. If recovery succeeds and the separate welcome roll wins, the event starts only then; if another app's PiP/Apple Music holds audio priority or the OS denies activation, keep the original visible retry/back behavior, do not launch random pets, fake recovery or silently take over audio. Confirm flashcards mic/Listen narration fix #93 still works, repeated attempted retries do not roll repeatedly, and no unusual game countdown/progress continues during a rescue. The odds may need several daily/profile simulations to visibly audition both kinds; the automated deterministic suite provides exact coverage. **Owner physical iPhone acceptance PENDING.**

## Flashcards post-recording native narration handoff correction (owner iPhone PENDING)

**Scoped fix:** The original Record tap already claimed foreground `.soloAmbient` ownership before input preparation. `devPrepareForMicrophone` deliberately marks native output as needing a new gesture while microphone `.playAndRecord` temporarily owns the same session. The existing Swift mic plugin only emits a private `biblozoo.flashcards.capture-restored` notification *after* a normally stopped/time-limited opaque RAM take is retained, original output category restored, and current app still owns foreground output without external audio takeover; it does so **before** the capture `ready:true` response allows Flashcards to render comparison. `BibloZooNativeSoundPlugin` consumes exactly one pending microphone handoff when actual interruption count (excluding delayed `wasSuspended` history), foreground epoch, media reset epoch, `!interrupted`, foreground activity, `.soloAmbient`, and no other audio all agree. It only clears the planned synthetic gesture gate; it never calls `setActive(true)`, changes category, resumes third-party music, creates a mic, exposes samples to JS, or fakes an audio `ended` event.

**Source/VM regression:** `node scripts/validate_flashcards_native_instruction_handoff.js` inspects exact Swift capture-post / output-observer / one-shot safety contracts, including invalidation on real `.began`, background, and media services loss/reset. It exercises the *actual* native narration JavaScript facade and Flashcards instruction controller with native-output bridge stubs: restored session → automatic single `flashcard-instruction-listen` prepare/play without new `activateFromGesture`; accepted native play does **not** unlock; only the matching `biblozooNarration(type:"ended")` unlocks; unrecovered interruption rejects without Web media fallback or unauthorized reactivation. CI runs this alongside pre-existing Flashcards N7c/N7d and instruction-audio checks. Linux Swift `-frontend -parse` checks syntax, **not Xcode Apple SDK type checking**. Both **Mac Xcode build and owner audible physical iPhone verification remain PENDING** until actually tested.

For each No Help/First Letters/Pictures: finish the level instructions, deliberately tap Record, observe unchanged 3→2→1 and active mic meter, tap Stop. Then tap Play My Recording and confirm actual recorded voice remains audible, no early Next/Wacky eligibility, no duplicate rewards. ## Flashcards instruction MP3 autoplay, disabled actions and measured comparison text (post-#91, owner iPhone PENDING)

**Assets added by owner** at native experiment head `0ee2cb2d6f24409d31ef5b99f9da0f7791172b12`: five local `flashcards/flashcard_instruction_*.mp3` files. Mapping is exact: No Help → `flashcard_instruction_no_help.mp3`, First Letters → `flashcard_instruction_first_letter.mp3`, Pictures → `flashcard_instruction_pictures.mp3`, the “Record yourself saying the verse...” page → `flashcard_instruction_record.mp3`, and “Listen to your recording, then compare it with the verse.” → `flashcard_instruction_listen.mp3`. Each plays once on entering its screen. Initial Next, Record, Skip Recording, Play My Recording, Re-record and Check Verse/Next actions are dimmed and disabled while that screen's instruction is pending; the browser path unlocks on real `ended`, the Audio Test iOS path on native `biblozooNarration(type:"ended")` through the existing `BibloZooNativeLearnTracks` player. A player prepare/accepted response or elapsed timer **must not** grant completion. **Skip Recording** reaches a different “Check the verse” screen; because there is no saved voice, do not play the Listen-to-your-recording clip there, or while microphone capture is pending. No automatic mic capture, recorded-voice playback, recording credit or Wacky unlock. Back/menu/exit remain available, cleanup stops narration, and task-switch never starts/restarts audio automatically; if narration fails or is interrupted, show “Try Instructions Again” and require a real retry tap. Deliberate mute skips the instruction rather than trapping a child behind silence.

**Native bundling/guard:** `scripts/build.js` already copies `flashcards/` into `dist/`; Capacitor packages it under `public/flashcards`. Swift `BibloZooNativeSoundPlugin.nativeNarrationURL` adds five exact allowlisted `flashcard-instruction-*` IDs to the existing `AVAudioPlayer` narration endpoint, not generic caller-selected filenames or a new audio session. The JS adapter only accepts those five in addition to its preexisting Learn/tutor IDs. The browser/PWA path uses the same MP3 files through ordinary media, with an explicit retry if autoplay is rejected; no HTML/WebAudio output fallback is allowed inside experimental iOS. Existing `.soloAmbient`, Apple Music/AirPods interruption, Flashcards opaque RAM-only capture/real recorded-take replay, privacy and rewards remain independently owned. Native recording Stop restores the session's prior category before the comparison screen requests instruction audio; actual post-capture audio success still needs a real device check.

**Text-fitting diagnosis and fix:** Prior `difficulty_intro` / `recording_intro` copy uses `fitRecordingInstructions()` to binary-fit roughly 1.375–2.75rem into its available card. The comparison message instead used a fixed CSS `clamp(1.5rem, min(7vw, 8vh), 3rem)` inside an unnecessarily narrow `width:min(100%,24ch)`. Replace that with measured `fitComparisonInstruction()` using the available comparison-stage size, a dedicated measured message span, and the same font range, rechecking after font load and resizing. The actual verse overlay's shared fitter remains separate and unchanged.

**Automated regression:** `node scripts/validate_flashcards_instruction_audio.js` verifies all five checked-in MP3s, strict native allowlists, native end vs ACK, duplicate-rerender suppression, failed/interrupted retry, stale callbacks, missing native bridge with no browser fallback, Web media ended, mute escape, disabled action guards, and comparison fitting source. ## N7m — Read My Verse Apple Music/AirPods/YouTube PiP Play must show Welcome Back (2026-11-11)

**Owner-specific reproduction:** With native-only Audio Test iPhone in **Read My Verse → Keyboard** and a **paused YouTube Picture-in-Picture** player, pressing **Play** in PiP starts YouTube but BibloZoo **does not pause or show Welcome Back**; Read sounds then stop and merely tapping the app does not recover them. Same with a remote **AirPods Play** starting an Apple Music MP3 while BibloZoo stays foreground. Owner suspects shared Read activities. These are *external audio-session takeovers while UIKit and WebKit remain visible*, not an app-background event.

**Verified source gap:** The native `BibloZooNativeReadOutputPlugin` receives `AVAudioSession.interruptionNotification` `.began`, but previously `cancelAll(reason:"interrupted")` notified only active `clips`; a silent Keyboard/Typewriter/cue gap or animated scene may own **zero players**, so the JS `onAppInactive` activity callback ran only for UIKit `willResignActive`, which never occurs for a foreground PiP/AirPods takeover. Shared `read_my_verse.js` already owns a standard fixed Welcome Back overlay, activity/cursor animation freeze, remaining timers, native player cleanup and explicit Let's go session recovery. Its `kind:"ack"` quiet path previously dismissed immediately before verifying native `activateFromGesture()` status, leaving a potentially silent ongoing activity.

**N7m native-only fix:** Swift emits fixed `biblozooReadLifecycle(phase:"audioInterruptionBegan")` for **actual** OS AVAudioSession `.began` even with no clips; genuine route loss/arrival produces `routeInterrupted`, media-services lost/reset `mediaServicesInterrupted`, preserving original held App Switcher `willResignActive`. The existing native Read JS adapter revokes activation/tone queues, invalidates clips and signals the one Read activity interruption owner for those exact phases; no arbitrary AVPlayer ended/category/paused-PiP false signal. Existing `pauseReadForInterruption` freezes Keyboard, Typewriter, Word Order, Unscramble, Chunk Matching, Balloons, Fish and Crawl through their *one* shared recovery. A real **Let's go!** still requests native `.soloAmbient` priority synchronously; native quiet/animated scene recovery waits for the **real OS activation decision** before dismissing Welcome Back/thawing clocks. Denied activation retains popup with retry + Back; no false native ended, scores/rewards, automatic foreground activation, `.mixWithOthers`/`.duckOthers`, mic, capture, Flashcards or Web Audio fallback. Web/PWA/Android behavior unchanged.

**Automated regression:** `node scripts/validate_native_read_external_audio_n7m.js` executes actual Read activity interruption and resume code with original native Read adapter; simulates external `.began` with **`document.hidden=false` and no AVAudioPlayer**; tests Keyboard/Typewriter and animated scenes, in-flight clip error vs ended, stale cues, post-interruption native denial/retry, repeated route/media/switcher notification idempotency, and delayed activation after a second interruption. Swift source check proves notify-before-teardown intent; workflow retains all N1–N7l checks, both Swift **syntax only** parsers and Web build. **Not a substitute for Apple SDK/Xcode or actual OS PiP/AirPods notification delivery and heard audio.**

**Owner iPhone checklist:** After review/merge, separate Mac Audio Test worktree `experiment/native-ios-audio`, Pull Origin → `npm run build && npx cap sync ios && npx cap open ios`, Xcode **App** `com.andydoane.biblozoo.audiotest`. (1) **Paused YouTube PiP** and active Keyboard: press PiP Play → visible, fixed **Welcome Back**, stopped cursor/timer. Press Let's go once; native session either truly returns or overlay remains with retry/Back. (2) **AirPods Play** starts external Apple Music MP3 during Keyboard → same popup without switching apps, ordinary app tap does NOT reclaim priority, real Let's go tries once. (3) Repeat across Typewriter, Balloons, Fish, Verse Crawl, Word Order, Unscramble, Chunk Matching, both *mid-voice* and *silent* stages. (4) Ensure a paused PiP alone, normal cue end or routine volume changes do NOT show popup. (5) Re-test N7f.2 half-held App Switcher/lock, N7g Flashcards/privacy and N7l pet unlock/onboarding. [Full N7m guide](N7M-READ-EXTERNAL-AUDIO-WELCOME-BACK.md). **N7m hardware owner PASS PENDING; actual emitted OS `.began` under user’s audio setup must be validated, not inferred from source/CI.** N7l #81 merged to native experiment at `2663b2a0d416ef329334367ce8869406554012cb`, [merge-head CI GREEN](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/actions/runs/43018077232), **owner reports pet-unlock/onboarding last PR working well** (targeted result; not broad external-audio acceptance). N7g full device pass and N7h wider Daily Questions hardware results remain separate.

## N7k — Learn Chunks/Echo quiet-phase task switch (Welcome Back, NO inline Start) — 2026-11-10

**Owner issue and reliable reproduction:** In **Learn → Echo**, iOS task-switch during **yellow text / silence while the child is speaking** reliably produced `Sound isn't ready. Tap Start to try again.` and a **Start** row that changed the activity's standard layout, instead of the existing **Welcome Back / Let's go!** overlay. Switching during spoken native audio normally showed the expected overlay. **Learn → Chunks** intermittently showed the same unexpected inline recovery, consistent with switching during the between-chunk silent gap.

**Source-confirmed cause:** N5b.3 `BibloZooNativeLearnTracks` UIKit lifecycle binding invalidates current active **AVAudioPlayer** and invokes the Learn interruption callback only when a native player is owned. Echo repeat windows and Chunks gap timers remain `State.echoRunning/chunkRunning`, but no native player is owned; JS `visibilitychange` may not fire promptly for the held App Switcher (N4a.1). Next unprimed narration can be mistaken for an asset/start failure and insert a Start/error row. The source does **not** prove a missing MP3.

**Experiment-only N7k fix:** A single activity-owned `biblozooAppLifecycle` observer calls the unchanged `pauseLearnAudioForInterruption` for *active* Chunks/Echo even with no native clip, including early UIKit inactive while `document.hidden=false`; late return/visibility are idempotent. A transient quiet checkpoint stores `chunk-gap` or `echo-repeat`, current chunk index and actual remaining time in the existing recovery object. A deliberate **Let's go** tap primes existing native `learn-part` audio and resumes the **same remaining silence**, rather than skipping/replaying a completed chunk. The fixed Welcome Back popup closes only after actual activity foreground-clock movement for quiet resume (this is *not* AVPlayer/speaker proof), or true native position/ended for active audio. No Learn completion/pass/reward based on a timer. True missing/refused asset continues to offer the original inline Start retry; interruption does not. Native-only iOS never invokes HTML/WebAudio; Web/PWA/Android and stable `ios-prep` untouched.

**New automated gate:** `node scripts/validate_native_learn_quiet_interruption_n7k.js` VM-executes **actual app.js Chunks/Echo run sequences**, 50ms quiet delay, activity Swift lifecycle observer, early held App Switcher, pause/Welcome Back/Let's go, saved remaining silence, same speaking yellow state, no lost/duplicate chunk or score, native interruption and actual failStart fallback. Wired into experimental GitHub Actions along with all N1–N7j checks, both Swift full-file grammar parsers and Web bundle build. **Linux CI is not Mac SDK compilation, actual speaker audibility or held UIKit interaction proof.**

**Owner real iPhone gate after merge:** Separate Mac Audio Test worktree on `experiment/native-ios-audio`, Pull Origin; run `npm run build && npx cap sync ios && npx cap open ios`, Xcode App on physical `com.andydoane.biblozoo.audiotest`. Echo yellow silent speaking turn: hold partial App Switcher / fully switch / lock; return shows Welcome Back **with unchanged layout**, Let's go restores *same* yellow speaking interval and then next native chunk, with no early grade/reward. Chunks: switch during a native clip AND repeat during very short gap; both fixed popup, no inline message/Start unless real sound failure. Test 5–10 repetitions, AirPods/external PiP priority, N7j in-app menu Close no false popup, original Web/PWA behavior. [Full N7k iPhone checklist](N7K-LEARN-CHUNKS-ECHO-QUIET-PHASE-WELCOME-BACK.md). **N7k owner iPhone PASS PENDING.** N7j #79 merged at `5adc7bfb9e6913f7747c7c82e402e49d4d0cb83c` with [merge-head Node/Web CI GREEN](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/actions/runs/42945186392); previous menu-specific hardware PASS not documented as exhaustive. ## N7g — Integrated audio reliability, bounded Read media ownership, Bluetooth/PiP/privacy (owner iPhone PENDING)

**Owner evidence at start (Nov 10, 2026):** The owner personally MERGED [N7f.2 PR #75](https://github.com/andydoane/biblozoo/pull/75) into isolated `experiment/native-ios-audio` at `b5a20006980ba8631dfb9c1b10a875fd8078413a`, tested on a real iPhone, and reports **things working well**. The previous held-App-Switcher Balloons/Fish/typing motion and waveform audition tickets are **CLOSED for the tested scope**. Owner auditioned the six alternatives and explicitly chooses to **KEEP existing native Unscramble and Tap Words in Order tones**. Do not promote new sounds. Previous #74 coral Flashcards toast merged, with no independently reported specific physical UI result. N6 12/12 standalone + Playground 5/5 and chosen Wacky D4 Haunted Whisper/C5 Wobbling Chorus remain accepted. **N7g complete cross-activity hardware signoff remains PENDING.**

**Actual source audit finding 1 — Read map growth:** `verse_games/shared/native-read-my-verse-output-n7f.js` previously added every `new NativeAudio(src)` to `clips` Map at construction, retaining unplayed and completed Typewriter/Keyboard wrappers, closures and events until global `stopAll`. If one lengthy activity created many key sounds, there was no per-clip release. N7g only registers upon **real `play()`**, removes owner on real native `ended`, interruption, pause, failure, lock/visibility, Exit. This does NOT change the native MP3 decoding, audio gain or sample choices; Flashcards recording and game progress are unchanged.

**Actual finding 2 — old end/clock on replay:** The same Audio object reused the same opaque request ID on its second/third native `playClip`, so a delayed previous native delegate `ended` or `progress` could mark a newly started clip heard or seek it to the previous end. N7g allocates **fresh `rmv-...` per play attempt**, removes older event-map owner immediately and keeps the old request ID for stale ACK stop only. Newly replaying an **already finished** clip resets native seek to 0 (original HTML media semantics); **paused/interrupted** Read media retains the current native clock until actual explicit Welcome Back/Resume Audio. `playClip(started:true)` still never means listened to completion, and a missing `AVAudioPlayerDelegate` event must not grant grade/Next/Zoo. A changed `Audio.src` or `setAttribute("src")` revalidates strict original fixed published-verse/sample IDs rather than silently replaying the preceding source.

**Actual finding 3 — async cue after Exit:** A Web authored Unscramble/Tap Order feedback cue could be queued while the owner `activateFromGesture()` promise was pending, then invoke `playCue` after an Exit, app switch, external AirPods Play or lock. New monotonic `lifecycleEpoch` invalidates the queued claim when `stopAll`, `visibilitychange`, native `willResignActive` or a new explicit activation occurs; genuine fresh child Start/Resume remains allowed. NO passive `.soloAmbient` `setActive`, no `.mixWithOthers`/`.duckOthers`, no WebAudio fallback/HTML output, file or microphone introduced. Flutter/Android/PWA original source audio behavior remains untouched.

**Automated gate:** New `node scripts/validate_native_n7g_integrated_audio_lifecycle.js` executes the real native Read JS in a VM with real async fake Capacitor: 500 created unplayed audio objects must register zero active owners; 40 short finished key clips must return registry size to zero; same Audio object complete→new Play emits fresh ID and replays from 0, stale ended/progress ignored, paused audio retains correct position, back/StopAll then deliberate new Start can reuse an object, changing src revalidates allowlist; an async cue following a revoked activation cannot play; a delayed native started ACK after Pause must stop only the old token; early willResignActive with `document.hidden=false` invalidates players before WebKit event. Source gates ensure owner-approved microphone RAM/discard, native delegate `.dataPlayedBack` Wacky, genuine route/Apple Music priority, no new audio session options/permissions and unchanged Web branch. Keep older N7f2/Read Patch20/70, N7f1 coral toast, N7e/Wacky, N7d2, N1–N6 and Zoo Node regressions plus both Swift whole-file `swiftc -frontend -parse` and production Web bundle CI. **Linux Swift frontend is NOT Mac iOS SDK typechecking, and Node does not prove hardware sound.**

**Owner hardware acceptance:** Merge only isolated `experiment/native-ios-audio`, Mac GitHub Desktop Audio Test worktree Pull origin → `npm run build && npx cap sync ios && npx cap open ios` → Xcode full App on physical iPhone `com.andydoane.biblozoo.audiotest`. Test repeat Flashcards true native RAM Record 3→2→1/16-bar meter/dry playback completion/Wacky once-only + D4/C5; re-record/skip/failed playback, coral toast; long Read My Verse sequence with many key/word sounds, completed clip replay from 0, interrupted explicit Resume from position not stale end; Balloon/Fish/Crawl held App Switcher and lock; switch Read→Flashcards→Learn/Feeding while sound active, no leaked old MP3 or chord; AirPods connect/disconnect + external Play/Apple Music/YouTube PiP audio arbitration, no automatic session seizure, no phantom Zoo/rewards or microphone prompt/file/backup. Check baseline N6 sound still works. Detailed [N7g owner checklist](N7G-INTEGRATED-AUDIO-RELIABILITY-PRIVACY-GATE.md) · [Audio recovery](AUDIO-RECOVERY.md) · [Living handoff](PROJECT-HANDOFF.md).
